### [1] [Anomaly inflow for simplicial Kähler fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21860)
*Simon Catterall*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三维流形有限三角剖分上传播的大量Kähler-Dirac费米子，通过分解Kähler场并施加特定边界条件，将Pfaffian分解为体部与边界部，并分析其在Z₄变换下的拓扑相位；发现边界异常可由体部反常流入抵消，且该机制在连续极限下对应引力Chern-Simons理论的反常流入及Wen-Zee项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kähler-Dirac费米子模型的反常结构，特别是边界上的’t Hooft反常及其由体部的反常流入抵消的机制。

Method: 将Kähler场分解为两个约化场，设定确保边缘模无质量的边界条件；对系统Pfaffian进行体部与边界部的因子分解，并分析其在全局Z₄变换下的拓扑相位；结合格点指标与连续场论（引力Chern-Simons理论、Wen-Zee项）解释反常流入。

Result: 证明体部与边界部各自贡献拓扑相位；边界非平凡相位标志’t Hooft反常；体部反常流入可抵消边界反常；对非定向边界，无体部时恢复拓扑超导体分类；连续极限下反常流入由引力Chern-Simons理论和Wen-Zee项描述。

Conclusion: 格点Kähler-Dirac系统能清晰刻画体-边反常匹配机制，为理解拓扑物态与引力反常提供了统一离散框架。

Abstract: We study massive Kähler Dirac fermions propagating on finite triangulations of various three dimensional manifolds with one or more boundaries. To probe the anomaly structure of these models we decompose the Kähler field into two reduced Kähler fields and choose boundary conditions that ensure that any edge modes are necessarily massless. We then show how to factorize the Pfaffian of this system into a product of bulk and boundary sectors each of which can separately pick up a phase under a global $Z_4$ transformation of the fermions that simultaneously changes the sign of the mass. These phases are topological, depending only on the index of the lattice Kähler operator in that sector. For the boundary sector a non-trivial phase signals the presence of a 't Hooft anomaly. We study cases where the boundary is anomalous but an inflow from the bulk cancels its phase. Canceling this anomaly for a non-orientable boundary in the absence of the bulk recovers the known classification of topological superconductors. We also give a continuum interpretation of these results in terms of inflow from a gravitational Chern Simons theory which is induced by integrating out massive bulk Kähler Dirac fermions. In the presence of a boundary the latter gives rise to Wen-Zee terms that mediate the anomaly inflow mechanism.

</details>

### [2] [Efficient and Optimal Analysis of Galaxy Surveys on Large Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21718)
*Julius Adolff,Uroš Seljak*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更快的场级似然方法，用于宇宙大尺度结构功率谱分析，尤其提升对原初非高斯性（local型）的约束能力，在DESI DR2数据上将f_NL的误差从4.8改进至4.1，并支持高效处理角向系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准功率谱估计器在最大尺度上因观测窗口导致模态耦合而次优；最优分析需协方差矩阵求逆，计算昂贵。

Method: 发展一种替代性的、计算更高效的场级似然方法，并引入一种新颖的非参数方法来缓解角向系统误差（如污染模态的高效去投影）。

Result: 相比标准功率谱分析，场级似然方法使原初非高斯性f_NL的约束能力提升14%；对DESI DR2 LRG和QSO样本，σ(f_NL)由4.8降至4.1。

Conclusion: 场级似然方法在保持信息完整性的同时显著提升计算效率与参数约束精度，尤其适用于大尺度、低信噪比及含系统误差的数据分析。

Abstract: On sufficiently large scales the galaxy overdensity field is approximately Gaussian, so its information can be losslessly compressed into the power spectrum. Standard power spectrum estimators are nevertheless suboptimal on the largest scales, where the survey window induces significant coupling between different modes. Optimal power spectrum analysis typically requires an inverse of the covariance matrix, which can be overly expensive. Here we develop an alternative field level approach, which is significantly faster. We assess its impact on constraining primordial non-Gaussianity of the local type, which is particularly sensitive to these largest scales. Relative to the standard power spectrum analysis, our field level likelihood forecasts a $14\%$ improvement in constraining power. For the DESI DR2 LRG and QSO samples, our approach forecasts $σ(f_{\rm NL})=4.1$, compared to $σ(f_{\rm NL})=4.8$ from the power spectrum based analysis. The field level approach also enables efficient implementations of additional non-cosmological contributions to the signal. As an example, we present a novel non-parametric method to mitigate angular systematics, enabling efficient deprojection of contaminated modes.

</details>


### [3] [Velocity Reconstruction from kSZ with SPT-3G and DESI-LS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21721)
*Selim C. Hotinli,Edmond Chaussidon,Simone Ferraro,Kendrick M. Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次利用南极望远镜（SPT）通过动力学Sunyaev-Zel'dovich（kSZ）效应重建大尺度径向速度场，并测量星系-速度关联及速度功率谱，结合多频段观测与前景建模，对原初非高斯性参数f_NL给出新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用kSZ效应重建宇宙大尺度结构的径向速度场，可提供独立于传统密度探针的动力学信息，用于检验宇宙学模型和原初非高斯性。

Method: 交叉相关SPT-3G D1在95/150/220 GHz三频段的CMB数据与DESI巡天的亮红星系样本；提取kSZ信号对应的星系-速度偶极/单极关联及速度功率谱；建模并分离热SZ和CIB等前景污染。

Result: 首次在220 GHz频段以显著性4.2（偶极）、3.1（单极）和3.8（功率谱）探测到重建速度；星系-速度关联信噪比达11.2，速度功率谱达8.1；对f_NL给出约束：-38^{+61}_{-61}（仅速度）和-20^{+40}_{-46}（联合星系分布）。

Conclusion: 成功实现基于SPT-3G的kSZ速度场重建，验证了多频kSZ观测可行性，为原初非高斯性和结构形成动力学提供了新观测约束。

Abstract: We reconstruct the large-scale radial velocity field from the kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect for the first time with the South Pole Telescope, and measure the galaxy-velocity correlation and the velocity power spectrum. We cross-correlate the SPT-3G D1 maps at 95, 150, and 220 GHz with luminous red galaxies from the DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys over the SPT-3G Main field. For the extended galaxy sample, we detect the galaxy-velocity correlation at signal-to-noise ratio $11.2$ and the velocity power spectrum at $8.1$, combining the three frequencies. We detect the reconstructed velocities for the first time at 220 GHz with signal-to-noise ratios of 4.2 and 3.1 from the galaxy-velocity correlation dipole and monopole, and at 3.8 from the velocity power spectrum. We model and estimate foreground contributions to the monopole, detecting both the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect and the cosmic infrared background contribution separately. On the around five percent of sky jointly covered by SPT-3G and DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys, reconstructed velocities give new constraints on local-type primordial non-Gaussianity at $f_{\rm NL}=-38^{+61}_{-61}$, and adding galaxy clustering gives $f_{\rm NL}=-20^{+40}_{-46}$. The galaxy-velocity correlation is consistent between the three frequencies.

</details>


### [4] [Baryonification V: Thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich maps from dark-matter-only lightcones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21733)
*Michael Kovač,Andrina Nicola,Jozef Bucko,Siyu Bian,Aurel Schneider,Robert Reischke,Jessica A. Cowell,Alexandre Refregier,Sambit K. Giri,Matthieu Schaller,Joop Schaye*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于暗物质N体模拟生成康普顿-y图（tSZ信号）的高效方法，利用组件式重子化（BFC）模型结合气体压力分布与水静力学平衡，校准于FLAMINGO模拟，实现了对tSZ功率谱~5–10%精度的预测，为欧几里得、LSST和西蒙斯天文台等巡天项目的弱引力透镜与tSZ联合模拟推断奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stage-IV弱引力透镜巡天中重子反馈是主要理论不确定性；tSZ效应可追踪反馈再分布的热气体，因此联合WL与tSZ有望标定反馈并提升宇宙学约束精度。但模拟推断需大量跨宇宙学与反馈模型的相关WL与y图，亟需高效可靠的tSZ前向建模方法。

Method: 提出两种基于暗物质N体光锥生成Compton-y图的方法：1）将压力剖面‘涂抹’到晕上；2）沿BFC重子化后的气体场重新分配y信号；BFC模型分别生成气体与恒星场，并由水静力学平衡计算气体压力；非热压力由FLAMINGO模拟气体速度直接测量而非设为自由参数；联合拟合气体质量分数与热压剖面完成模型校准。

Result: 预测的y剖面在晕间散射范围内匹配FLAMINGO；在FLAMINGO晕上绘制时，对两种AGN反馈模型的tSZ功率谱在ℓ≈500–3000范围内再现精度达~5%；应用于CosmoGridV1类N体模拟时，在ℓ≈300–8000范围内与FLAMINGO偏差约10%，差异主要源于数百个最亮星系团，剔除后二者一致。

Conclusion: 该方法能从大规模N体模拟（如CosmoGridV1）高效、高精度生成tSZ前向模型，显著降低对全流体模拟的依赖，是实现Euclid、LSST和Simons Observatory等项目WL+tSZ联合模拟推断的关键技术进展。

Abstract: Baryonic feedback is a major theoretical uncertainty for Stage-IV weak lensing (WL) surveys. The thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (tSZ) effect traces the hot gas redistributed by feedback, so combining it with WL can calibrate feedback and improve cosmological constraints. Doing so with simulation-based inference requires correlated lensing and Compton-$y$ maps across many cosmologies and feedback scenarios. We present two methods to generate Compton-$y$ maps from dark-matter-only $N$-body light cones using the component-wise baryonification (BFC) model, which produces separate gas and stellar fields and computes the gas pressure from hydrostatic equilibrium. The two methods, painting pressure profiles onto halos or redistributing the signal along the baryonified gas, agree to within a few per cent. We calibrate the model to the fiducial and strongest AGN feedback FLAMINGO simulations by jointly fitting gas mass fractions and thermal pressure profiles, measuring the non-thermal pressure from the simulated gas velocities rather than treating it as a free parameter. The predicted Compton-$y$ profiles match FLAMINGO within halo-to-halo scatter, and when painted onto the FLAMINGO halos we reproduce the tSZ power spectrum of both feedback models to $\sim5\%$ for $500\lesssim\ell\lesssim3000$. Applied to a \emph{CosmoGridV1-like} $N$-body simulation, our maps agree with FLAMINGO within $\sim10\%$ for $300\lesssim\ell\lesssim8000$; the remaining differences come from the few hundred most massive clusters, and removing them from both halo catalogs brings the two into agreement. Our method thus enables accurate and efficient tSZ forward models from $N$-body simulation suites such as CosmoGridV1, an important step towards joint WL and tSZ simulation-based inference for Euclid, LSST, and the Simons Observatory.

</details>


### [5] [The phase gradient spectrum: probing the squeezed bispectrum of the 21-cm signal during reionization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21760)
*Sambit K. Giri,P. Daniel Meerburg,Léon V. E. Koopmans*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的统计量——相位梯度谱，用于高效探测再电离时期21-cm信号的非高斯性，其物理含义为角平均归一化压扁三谱，可反映小尺度功率对长波扰动的响应；该统计量在模拟中展现出特征性的非单调演化，并能显著提升SKA-Low对天体物理参数的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21-cm再电离信号高度非高斯，传统功率谱忽略傅里叶相位信息，而高阶统计（如双谱）计算昂贵且信息分散，亟需一种高效、物理意义明确的新统计量。

Method: 构建并理论推导‘相位梯度谱’，定义为其测量傅里叶模式间相位差的圆周相干性；通过解析论证其等价于角平均归一化压扁双谱；在多种再电离模拟中分析其演化行为；结合SKA-Low进行Fisher信息预测以评估参数约束能力。

Result: 相位梯度谱展现出特征性非单调演化：早期追踪物质密度场非高斯性，中段（⟨x_HI⟩ ≲ 0.9）转而反映电离拓扑结构，相位相干性在再电离中期达最小值（对应压扁双谱变号），后期随中性岛收缩而回升；1000小时SKA-Low观测即可单独约束天体物理参数，10000小时使约束提升≥2倍；联合功率谱后，边缘化参数约束进一步提升约20%。

Conclusion: 相位梯度谱是一种高效、物理直观且信息丰富的统计工具，能有效提取21-cm再电离信号中的非高斯信息，在当前及未来射电阵列观测中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: The 21-cm signal from the epoch of reionization is highly non-Gaussian. Standard analyses rely on the power spectrum, which is blind to the Fourier phases, while higher-order statistics such as the bispectrum are computationally expensive and spread information across triangle configurations. As an efficient alternative, we develop the \textit{phase gradient spectrum}, which measures the circular coherence of phase differences between Fourier modes separated by a long-wavelength mode. We show analytically that this statistic measures the angle-averaged normalised squeezed bispectrum, which describes the response of small-scale power to long-wavelength fluctuations. Applied to reionization simulations with different ionizing source properties, the phase gradient spectrum of the 21-cm signal shows a characteristic non-monotonic evolution. At early times, the spectrum tracks the non-Gaussianity of the matter density field. Once the mean neutral fraction $\langle x_\mathrm{HI}\rangle \lesssim 0.9$, the spectrum follows the ionization topology. The phase coherence reaches a minimum midway through reionization and rises again as the remaining neutral regions shrink into isolated islands. Statistics that keep the sign of the squeezed bispectrum show that this minimum marks its sign change. This evolution holds for different reionization models and simulation frameworks. A Fisher forecast for a 1000-h Square Kilometre Array (SKA)-Low observation, combining five redshifts across reionization, shows that the phase gradient spectrum alone constrains the astrophysical parameters, and these constraints tighten by a factor of $\gtrsim 2$ for a 10,000-h observation. Combining the phase gradient spectrum with the power spectrum tightens the marginalised constraints by up to $\sim$20 per cent relative to the power spectrum alone.

</details>


### [6] [Cross-correlating the SPHEREx extragalactic background light with CMB lensing I: theoretical predictions and forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21779)
*Azadeh Moradinezhad Dizgah,Zucheng Gao,Tom Chiesura*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用SPHEREx卫星观测近红外河外背景光（EBL）与CMB透镜效应交叉相关来探测星系积分光（IGL）、晕内光（IHL）及发射线的可行性，发现连续谱建模精度对发射线探测信噪比至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外EBL包含星系发射线叠加在更亮的积分星系光（IGL）和晕内光（IHL）连续谱之上；直接提取发射线面临视线方向滤波损失大尺度模式、以及连续谱作为相关前景干扰两大挑战。需发展能同时分离并测量各成分的稳健方法。

Method: 基于统一晕模型框架，对SPHEREx深场与全天巡天数据，结合Planck与Simons Observatory（SO）量级的CMB透镜图，开展EBL各成分（IGL、IHL、多条发射线）交叉功率谱的可探测性预报；系统评估不同滤波策略、连续谱先验知识（已知形状、用自功率谱校准、仅靠交叉谱拟合）对信噪比的影响。

Result: 未滤波且联合拟合时：Planck透镜下深场IGL达9σ探测但发射线不可见；全天巡天中IGL/IHL精度达1.0%/3%，五条线联合模板达4.6σ（SO提升至7.4σ）；径向滤波（>600 Mpc）使所有成分信噪比损失超90%；连续谱形状已知时线探测最强；用自功率谱校准后仍保持7.3–7.4σ；仅靠交叉谱拟合则降至1.4–2.3σ；不同线模型间探测显著性相差7–11倍。

Conclusion: EBL与CMB透镜的交叉相关是测量IGL的鲁棒探针，亦为探测发射线的可行途径，但前提是必须在全部光谱通道上精确建模连续谱；单纯滤波无法有效提取发射线，关键在于连续谱参数的联合拟合与校准。

Abstract: The near-infrared extragalactic background light (EBL), which SPHEREx maps in 102 spectral channels, holds the emission lines of galaxies on top of a much brighter continuum from the integrated galaxy light (IGL) and the intrahalo light (IHL). Cross-correlating these maps with the lensing of the cosmic microwave background (CMB) isolates the emission that traces the matter, unbiased by uncorrelated foregrounds. The lines face two obstacles. Filtering along the line of sight removes the long radial modes that carry the cross-correlation, and the extragalactic continuum, a signal in its own right, is a correlated foreground for the lines. We forecast the detectability of all EBL components, in a common halo-model framework, for the SPHEREx deep fields and all-sky survey with lensing maps at the sensitivity level of Planck and the Simons Observatory (SO). Without filtering, and with all amplitudes fitted jointly, the cross-correlation with Planck lensing detects the IGL at $9σ$ in the deep fields, but not the lines. In the all-sky survey, it measures the IGL and IHL amplitudes to $1.0\%$ and $3\%$ and detects the five lines, fitted as one template, at $4.6σ$. With SO lensing, these become $0.6\%$, $1.6\%$ and $7.4σ$. In these joint fits, a filter removing radial modes longer than about $600$ Mpc costs every component $90\%$ or more of its signal-to-noise ratio, so the lines are reached by fitting the continuum, not by filtering it. These values assume a known spectral shape of the continuum. Calibrating it with the continuum's auto-spectra keeps the lines at $7.3$--$7.4σ$; fitting it from the cross-spectrum alone leaves $1.4$--$2.3σ$. The line detections vary by a factor of seven to eleven across line models. The cross-correlation is thus a robust probe of the IGL and a viable route to the emission lines, provided the continuum is modeled across the channels.

</details>


### [7] [Cross-correlating the SPHEREx extragalactic background light with CMB lensing II: theoretical predictions confronted with lightcone simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21780)
*Azadeh Moradinezhad Dizgah,Zucheng Gao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟SPHEREx强度图与CMB透镜收敛场的交叉相关，研究近红外波段中集成星系光（IGL）、晕内光（IHL）及发射线对暗物质分布的示踪能力，验证了无自由参数的晕模型在功率谱预测上的高精度，并评估了不同成分的信噪比及滤波影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确建模近红外强度映射中的集成星系光（IGL）、晕内光（IHL）和发射线，是预测可探测性并正确解读CMB透镜交叉相关观测结果的关键；需区分共同起源于暗物质分布的信号与不相关前景和噪声。

Method: 基于Hidden Valley N体模拟构建z≈6、100 deg²天区的光锥，用恒星种群合成法为每个晕分配连续谱与发射线（两种金属丰度模型），生成SPHEREx波段强度图及对应CMB透镜收敛场；采用无自由参数的晕模型预测功率谱，并与模拟结果对比；结合SPHEREx深场与Simons Observatory透镜噪声评估信噪比及径向模式滤波效果。

Result: 晕模型在2.2 μm波段对IGL和发射线自功率谱及其与透镜场交叉谱的预测误差分别控制在5%（ℓ>400）和7%（ℓ>200）以内；线性理论仅适用于发射线交叉谱至ℓ≈1000–1600，而IGL与透镜场在ℓ~几百时即需非线性处理；未滤波下各成分交叉信噪比达9–21（发射线）、20（IGL）、15（IHL）；600 Mpc径向滤波保留62% IGL信噪比但抑制线信号至≤20%，振幅拟合仍保持~1σ精度；发射模型改变通道强度达3倍，但线探测性仅变化数十个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了基于物理晕模型的强度映射交叉相关分析的可行性与鲁棒性，为SPHEREx等未来巡天利用近红外强度映射探测大尺度结构及暗物质分布提供了关键建模基础与观测预期。

Abstract: Cross-correlating intensity maps with the lensing of the cosmic microwave background (CMB) rejects the foregrounds and noise that do not trace the dark matter. In the near infrared, the remaining signal combines the integrated galaxy light (IGL), the intrahalo light (IHL) and several emission lines. Accurate modeling of all three is essential to forecast detectability and interpret measurements. We present simulated SPHEREx intensity maps of all three on a lightcone, painted on halos from the Hidden Valley $N$-body simulation, and the CMB-lensing convergence of the same lightcone, over $100\,{\rm deg}^2$ to $z \approx 6$. We assign each halo its lines and continuum by stellar-population synthesis, in two models whose metallicities are fixed or follow its stellar mass. Halo-model predictions from the simulation's luminosity-halo mass relations and measured clustering, without free parameters, match the auto-spectra of the IGL and lines, and their cross-spectra with lensing, across the band: at $2.2\,μ$m, to within $5\%$ above $\ell \approx 400$ and $7\%$ above $\ell = 200$, respectively. The agreement requires nonlinear clustering: linear theory describes the cross-spectrum of a line up to $\ell \approx 1000$-$1600$ at $2.2\,μ$m, but fails for the IGL and the convergence at multipoles of a few hundred. Abstract: We describe and test a new numerical method to solve the Vlasov/Boltzmann equation in systems with arbitrary levels of collisionality. The method combines recent ideas to evolve the distribution function (DF) directly in 6+1 dimensional phase-space in a manner which is Lagrangian in both position+velocity. The 'particles' of traditional Monte-Carlo/Particle-In-Cell/N-body (MC/PIC/NB) methods become mesh-generating points defining a volume partition, each of which carries a set of momentum-Lagrangian basis functions representing a local anisotropic momentum DF. The DF is then integrated between cells and bases via a higher-order reconstruction and Godunov-type method, evolving a 10 or 13-moment representation of each basis to ensure manifest mass/momentum/energy conservation. The method allows one to represent arbitrary DFs, including multiple independent streams but also collisional or phase-mixed or thermal distributions (unlike traditional 'moments' or waterbag/tessellation methods), and the cost is bounded to a modest multiplier of MC/PIC/NB costs. We provide an example implementation which uses a spatially-Lagrangian volume partition, second-order reconstruction, and simple set of bases which ensure constant cell masses and couple directly to existing MC/PIC/NB machinery, made public in the code GIZMO. We validate in a series of test problems, demonstrating manifest conservation, Galilean and translation and rotation invariance, and ability to represent complex DFs without spurious dissipation or instability. We discuss applications where this method can have advantages compared to traditional algorithms for simulations of dark matter, stellar, weakly-collisional neutral gas, and particulate/dust dynamics; multiphase/polydisperse flows; cosmic ray and radiation transport/hydrodynamics; and plasma physics.

Abstract: Deviations from general relativity on cosmological scales have been proposed as possible explanations for dark energy and dark matter. Following our selection cuts, the sample comprises 85 818 clusters at $0.1\leq z \leq0.4$ with $M_{\rm 500c}\gtrsim 6\times10^{13}\,M_\odot$. To enable a robust clustering analysis of this sample, we develop and validate methods for constructing random catalogues and calibrating the effective halo bias. We model the redshift-space two-point correlation function monopole and quadrupole over separations of $10$--$150\,h^{-1}\mathrm{Mpc}$ to jointly constrain gravitational growth and Alcock--Paczynski (AP) distortions, fixing the matter power-spectrum shape to that of a fiducial cosmology. We infer the product $fσ_8$ of the linear growth rate $f$ and the matter power spectrum normalisation $σ_8$, obtaining $fσ_8=0.45\pm0.08$ at an effective redshift of $z_{\rm eff}=0.28$, changing to $0.52\pm0.03$ when excluding baryon acoustic oscillation scales with tight AP priors. Abstract: The rapid growth of radio astronomical surveys has created a major challenge in identifying scientifically interesting sources from large populations of observed objects. This study investigates whether enhanced Isolation Forest (iForest) methods can improve the early detection and ranking of scientifically interesting radio sources. The methods are evaluated using Average Precision (AP), receiver operating characteristic area under the curve (ROC AUC), Precision@10, Precision@50, and cumulative anomaly detection. Pooled Gain achieves the highest Average Precision (AP = 0.201) and the strongest early-stage ranking performance (P@10 = 0.600), identifying six expert-validated anomalies among the ten highest-ranked sources. Standard Enhanced and Kurtosis-based achieve the highest ROC AUC (0.677), while the AUC values of the four variants range from 0.653 to 0.677. These results indicate that feature-aware isolation strategies can improve the prioritization of scientifically interesting sources at the top of the ranked list, although overall discrimination remains comparable across the evaluated variants. The findings support the use of enhanced Isolation Forest methods as an initial ranking mechanism within human-in-the-loop systems for large-scale radio astronomical surveys.

Abstract: Smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) simulations are frequently post-processed on Cartesian grids for applications such as radiative transfer calculations, visualisation, and data analysis. We present a new finite-volume algorithm designed to satisfy these requirements simultaneously. Starting from a finite-volume formulation of the SPH density field, we derive a simple expression for the mass contained in a Cartesian voxel in terms of a cumulative kernel function. The cumulative kernel is tabulated numerically once for any compact-support smoothing kernel, independently of its analytical form, so that the same tabulation and voxelisation machinery applies unchanged to any such kernel. During voxelisation, the mass contributions are obtained from interpolated evaluations of this precomputed function at voxel corners, with values at corners shared by neighbouring voxels evaluated only once and reused. We compare the new cumulative-kernel algorithm to nearest-grid-point assignment, centre sampling, regular subsampling, and Monte Carlo integration, using direct high-order numerical integration as a reference for the voxel densities, and implement it in the 3D Monte Carlo radiative transfer code SKIRT. The cumulative-kernel algorithm reproduces the reference voxel densities substantially more accurately than the sampling-based methods considered. Abstract: Our ability to understand the highest energy processes and environments in astronomy is directly tied to our ability to probe their physical processes at the smallest scales. Despite transformative progress in high-angular resolution instruments across the radio through optical, angular resolution within the X-rays has plateaued around $\sim0.1-1''$, and therefore our window into the innermost regions of the high energy Universe lies primarily within spectral, timing, and/or polarimetric studies rather than direct imaging. This represents a significant barrier to gaining a robust understanding of key science questions, including the mechanisms of accretion and jet launching, the accretion activity and angular momentum losses of binary active galactic nuclei (AGN) leading to gravitational wave events, and the space weather events that may influence atmospheric evolution and prebiotic chemistry on potentially habitable exoplanets. The Accretion Explorer Interferometer (AEI) is a mission concept for X-ray imaging that could achieve up to $μ$as resolution, $\sim5-6$ orders of magnitude better than current instrumentation. Here we present the scientific discovery space enabled by such an advance, and highlight areas of uncertainty and open questions that are critical to investigate for future development of this concept.

Image restoration with phase diversity (PD) and multi-frame blind deconvolution (MFBD) techniques lead to remarkable improvement in image quality, but is limited by the number of aberration parameters that can be determined. Compensation for the missing high-order modes is needed to further improve image quality, and to compensate for residual straylight. Aims. We aim to improve on methods for compensating missing high-order aberrations previously proposed by Scharmer et al. (2010) and Lofdahl & Hillberg (2022). Methods. The wavefront is divided into a set of low-order aberrations, which are treated individually, and high-order aberrations that are treated collectively. The high-order part of the transfer function is averaged, assuming Gaussian seeing statistics with Kolmogorov turbulence for the individual aberrations. Assuming that their accumulated effect is roughly uniform over the pupil, we obtain a high-order modulation transfer function that is represented with a single free parameter. Results. The high quality of the of the proposed high-order modulation transfer function is verified by calculating the Strehl from random samples of point-spread functions for D/r0 ranging from 4 to 20, where D is the telescope diameter and r0 the Fried seeing parameter. The proposed approximation offers significant advantages over the earlier proposals. Conclusions. The proposed method of compensating for missing high-order aberrations in PD or MFBD image reconstruction can dramatically improve the fidelity of solar images. Variations of the seeing (r0) within a data-set can be compensated for self consistently during PD or MFBD processing, but the average value of r0 must be provided from an external source. The combined dataset is assembled in under two hours, and the recovered amplitude and spectral index of the common process are consistent with the published IPTA DR2 result. Abstract: Wormhole solutions in General Relativity can only exist in the presence of exotic matter that violates the null energy condition. Abstract: Runaway supermassive black holes (SMBHs) have long been predicted. RBH-1, discovered with HST and confirmed by JWST, provides the strongest evidence yet for a runaway SMBH: a bow shock and linear trail in the circumgalactic medium (CGM) of a $z=0.96$ galaxy. The bow shock is $\sim$kpc wide, the trail is $\sim 60$ kpc long, and the SMBH apparently moves at $\sim 1,000$ km s$^{-1}$. Using controlled hydrodynamic simulations, we show that the SMBH's gravity alone cannot produce a significant bow shock, but a comoving wind source can sustain one similar to RBH-1's. The wind must either be active or have shut off less than $\sim 3$ Myr ago. The required recent wind momentum flux is $6\times10^{32}\,{\rm dyn}\,(n_{\rm CGM}/10^{-3}\,{\rm cm}^{-3})$, where $n_{\rm CGM}$ is the CGM density. Assuming the wind reaches peak strength only during the last $\sim 6$ Myr, the minimum integrated wind mass is $3.5\times10^5\,M_\odot\,(n_{\rm CGM}/10^{-3}\,{\rm cm}^{-3})$. A $2\times10^7\,M_\odot$ SMBH accreting at $1\%$ Eddington could power the wind. Supernova feedback from a star cluster carried by the SMBH is an alternative, although the required mass and momentum fluxes $\gtrsim 60$ Myr after ejection are difficult to reconcile with a standard initial mass function. For a hot ambient CGM at $\sim 10^6$ K, the quasi-linear trail constrains the 1D velocity dispersion to $σ_{\rm 1D}\lesssim 30$ km s$^{-1}$, above which it becomes strongly disturbed. Runaway SMBH trails therefore offer a new probe of turbulence in the diffuse CGM, in regimes where its kinematics are otherwise inaccessible.

Alternatively, wandering MBHs can be added through mergers. The growth of the most massive MBHs is insensitive to the seeding threshold. While the total number of MBH mergers varies by an order of magnitude, the number of massive dynamically resolved MBH mergers differs by most a factor of $\sim3$ between seeding thresholds. Abstract: Using APOGEE red clump stars and open clusters with precise ages, we investigate the age--metallicity relation (AMR) across the Galactic disk as a function of Galactocentric radius $R$ and vertical height $|Z|$. For stars older than $\sim$6--8 Gyr, the AMR shows little dependence on either $R$ or $|Z|$ within the uncertainties, suggesting rapid and well-mixed chemical evolution in the early disk. In contrast, younger populations exhibit a clear negative radial metallicity gradient, with the inner disk being more metal-rich than the outer disk, consistent with inside-out disk growth since $\sim$6--8 Gyr ago. Most importantly, at fixed location, the AMR for ages $\lesssim6$--8 Gyr is non-monotonic: [Fe/H] decreases toward younger ages, reaches a minimum at intermediate ages, and then rises again. Beyond $R\sim11$\,kpc, the relation becomes even more complex, exhibiting an additional reversal and a distinct metallicity peak at an age of $\sim2$\,Gyr. A toy model incorporating inside-out growth, two gas-inflow episodes, and radial migration qualitatively reproduces the observed trends, highlighting the combined roles of episodic gas accretion and radial migration in shaping the chemical evolution of the Galactic disk.

Abstract: We study the local regulation of star formation efficiency (SFE) in star-forming regions of nearby galaxies using spatially resolved data from the Mapping Nearby Galaxies at Apache Point Observatory (MaNGA) and the ALMA-MaNGA QUEnching and STar formation (ALMaQUEST) surveys. Furthermore, the relationship between SFE and shear varies among galaxies and weakens in gas-rich regions, suggesting that gas density may modulate the impact of shear. This behavior is consistent with competition between shear-induced cloud stabilization and shear-driven cloud-cloud collisions. Overall, galactic shear appears to regulate the large-scale radial distribution of SFE, while cloud-scale interstellar medium physics may drive the remaining variation.

Abstract: Little red dots (LRDs) combine V-shaped ultraviolet-optical continua, broad Balmer emission, and weak X-rays. The nearby analog SDSS J102530.29+140207.3, known as Lord/Egg, offers detailed spectroscopy and polarimetry. We test one active galactic nucleus (AGN) geometry by fitting its ultraviolet-to-infrared continuum with an inclination-dependent accretion-disk spectral energy distribution (SED), young stars, and a clumpy dusty screen. The same SED is used in Cloudy calculations of a radially stratified, virialized broad-line region (BLR). The fit favors log10(M_BH/M_sun) about 7.4, highly super-Eddington accretion, inclination above 70 degrees, and line-of-sight cloud extinction of about 3 mag. It also predicts a 2 keV luminosity below the Chandra limit. A BLR whose density rises from 10^9 to 10^11 cm^-3 near the sublimation radius reproduces the broad H-alpha profile without a separate intermediate-width Gaussian. For cloud columns near 10^23 cm^-2, it also matches the different Balmer-line widths, exponential-like wings, and Balmer decrement without electron scattering. The redshifted H-beta-H-delta absorber lacks an H-alpha counterpart, requiring velocity-coherent H-alpha re-emission into the absorption trough. Cool outer clumps explain K I and saturated Na I absorption, while the geometry recovers the continuum and H-alpha polarization and their position-angle offset. Ca II triplet absorption requires localized, non-radiatively heated, warm dust-poor zones near the sublimation front. He I absorption traces a fast, about 500 km s^-1, low-column ionized BLR skin. All projected absorber velocities remain below the local escape speeds, consistent with a bound failed BLR wind. Thus, a dusty, high-inclination accretion system can explain Lord/Egg within one connected geometry.

Abstract: Bent-tailed radio galaxies have traditionally been associated with, and used as tracers of, the dense intracluster medium. In recent years, however, such sources have also been identified outside of clusters, with only a few discovered in low-density environments of cosmic filaments. Their occurrence in low-density environments remains rare and is still poorly understood. We announce the discovery of bent-tailed radio galaxies in galaxy cluster filaments. We have identified two bent-tailed radio galaxies, J0020-25 and J0019-26, within a filament at the redshift of $z \sim 0.1273$, and another bent-tailed radio galaxy, J0019-25, in a separate filament at $z \sim 0.1126$. We used MeerKAT observations of these bent-tailed radio galaxies to place constraints on their intrafilament medium (IFM). The effects of the filaments could be seen in the radio morphologies of these galaxies. Using faint regions in each galaxy, we estimate the intrafilament pressure, density, magnetic field strength, and total energy density. We found an IFM density of $ρ_\text{IFM} \approx 3.8\times 10^{-29}$ g cm$^{-3}$ for the surrounding medium of J0020-25 and J0019-26, and $ρ_\text{IFM} \approx (0.2 - 1.1) \times 10^{-26}$ g cm$^{-3}$ around J0019-25. We found that our estimates for the intrafilament environment around J0020-25 and J0019-26 lie within the $ρ_\text{IFM}$ literature estimates, while the density estimate found using J0019-25 is higher than the literature density estimates for the IFM and in agreement with the density of group environments. J0019-25 likely resides in a galaxy group embedded within a filament. These discoveries show the capabilities of using bent-tailed radio galaxies as probes to locate the otherwise invisible large-scale filaments.

Hierarchical assembly predicts that galaxy mergers drive gas to galactic nuclei and bring BHs together, leaving a direct observational signature: dual AGN at tens of kpc separations and sub-kpc to few-kpc "duets" in post-merger galaxies, which are the progenitors of the low-frequency GW sources targeted by pulsar timing arrays and LISA. JWST has opened the window to study these systems at z>3. However, these early discoveries are very limited number and come from heterogeneous, largely serendipitous samples, so these systems can hardly be used for robust statistical studies. Within the Genesis initiative We propose a uniformly selected census of dual AGN and their hosts from cosmic noon to cosmic dawn, as a science case for the Genesis initiative. Our tiered strategy uses the survey imaging and spectroscopy for candidate selection at no extra cost, followed by NIRSpec IFU confirmation, multi-band NIRCam (and MIRI/ALMA) host characterisation, and multi-epoch revisits. Confirming and characterising about 100 pairs requires of order 1000 hr, well beyond the ceiling of standard TAC programs, and demands multi-cycle continuity. The census will deliver pair fractions as functions of redshift, separation and luminosity, BH masses and mass ratios, host properties and environments, and the empirical anchor needed to turn merger models into realistic LISA rate forecasts.

Method: 结合Euclid光学/近红外与LOFAR低频射电大天区巡天数据，在Euclid深场北区构建高置信度RLAGN样本；严格处理交叉证认可靠性与恒星质量完备性；区分低激发射电星系（LERG）与宿主星系的星暴/宁静性质，并分析不同红移、不同星系类型下的出现率–恒星质量关系。

Conclusion: LERG的宿主质量依赖性随红移显著演化，支持其供能机制从早期冷气体吸积/并合主导，过渡到本地需大质量热晕供能的演化图景。

Abstract: The incidence of radio-loud active galactic nuclei (RLAGN) and their energetic output via AGN feedback are crucial in understanding the evolution of galaxies and the large-scale structure of the Universe. In the local Universe, the incidence of RLAGN has been shown to correlate strongly with host galaxy properties, particularly stellar mass. Using the large-area survey capabilities provided by Euclid and the LOw Frequency ARray (LOFAR), we present the first study of the RLAGN incidence--stellar mass relation to $z=3.0$. We perform a stringent analysis accounting for cross-matching reliability and stellar-mass incompleteness. In addition, we test the robustness of our results against AGN and host galaxy classification. This yields a sample of $3\,154$ RLAGN in the Euclid Deep Field North, comprising $2\,803$ low-excitation radio galaxies (LERGs). We find that the LERG incidence as a function of stellar mass and radio luminosity follows a tight correlation over more than two orders of magnitude in stellar mass to $z=3.0$. The slope of the power-law relation decreases from $γ\approx2.5$ in the local Universe to $γ\approx1.0$ at $z=3.0$, indicating a relative increase in low-mass LERGs at earlier epochs. Separating the LERG population into actively star-forming and quiescent galaxies reveals that quiescent LERGs show a much steeper relation between incidence and stellar mass, with $γ\approx2.5$ compared to star-forming LERGs that show $γ\approx1.5$. However, the relation seems to be independent of whether galaxies are star-forming or quiescent at $z\geq2$, where both populations show relatively flat relations with $γ\approx1.0$. Our results suggest an evolutionary picture in which the LERG population transitions from cold-mode, merger-dominated accretion at earlier epochs to a scenario in which systems require a massive hot halo to fuel the AGN in the local Universe.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学分光光度法能直接揭示星系和类星体的物理条件、运动学和形成历史，但需要高效、准确的分析工具来处理DESI海量光谱数据。

Method: 采用前向建模方法，结合恒星种群合成与发射线模板，并卷积光纤的本征光谱分辨率；利用蒙特卡洛重采样估计不确定性。

Result: 实现了对3800万个红移0<z<4的河外天体的光谱测光及物理参数测量，包括恒星速度弥散、恒星形成历史、恒星质量、尘埃衰减等，并发布了DR2增值星表及配套测光星表。

Conclusion: FastSpecFit具备高精度、高效率和可扩展性，已成为DESI数据处理的关键工具，为大样本星系演化研究提供了可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Optical spectrophotometry provides a direct window into the physical conditions, kinematics, and formation histories of galaxies and quasars. FastSpecFit is an open-source Python software package that forward-models each Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) spectrum jointly with its broadband photometry, convolving stellar-population synthesis and emission-line templates with each fiber's native spectral resolution. This forward-modeling approach preserves DESI's uncorrelated pixel statistics, fits a typical spectrum in ~0.1 s of single-core time, and scales to tens of millions of objects, with uncertainties estimated via Monte Carlo resampling. FastSpecFit measures stellar velocity dispersions, non-parametric star-formation histories, stellar masses, star-formation rates, dust attenuation, K-corrections, rest-frame magnitudes, and fluxes and equivalent widths for 46 emission lines, including separate narrow and broad components for the Balmer lines. We describe these algorithms in detail, validate the measurements against independent codes, and present the resulting value-added catalog for DESI Data Release 2, which provides spectrophotometric and physical properties for 38 million extragalactic targets at 0<z<4. We also release a companion photometry-only catalog and document the catalogs previously released for the DESI Early Data Release and Data Release 1.

</details>

<details>

Method: 基于JWST/NIRCam与NIRISS三个观测项目的近红外图像，开展定制化数据处理，构建覆盖核心至两倍半光半径（10′）的深度测光与天体测量星表（oMEGACat-JWST），并评估其完备性、天体测量精度及色-星等图特征。

Result: 获得约67.7万个源的星表；在与原oMEGACat重叠区恢复>90%的HST源，并新增超8.6万个HST不可见的暗弱源；单幅图像天体测量精度达0.15 mas（F115W=18）至1 mas（F115W=23）；CMD清晰分辨氦丰度子群并敏感反映暗弱恒星的CNO元素变化。

Conclusion: oMEGACat-JWST是目前ω Cen核心最完备的测光星表，结合HST数据可实现多波段恒星子群分析，其高精度天体测量将助力未来对中心高速恒星轨道及潜在中等质量黑洞的研究。

Abstract: Omega Centauri (NGC 5139, $ω$ Cen) is the most massive globular cluster in the Milky Way and the likely stripped nucleus of an accreted dwarf galaxy. Extensive HST observations have uncovered many of its peculiar features, including complex subpopulations and kinematic evidence for an intermediate-mass black hole via central fast-moving stars. High-precision astrometric follow-up is essential to constrain the orbits of these stars. We present oMEGACat-JWST, a deep photometric and astrometric catalog of $ω$ Cen's core and surroundings that extends our previous oMEGACat efforts to near-infrared wavelengths. The superior sensitivity and angular resolution of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) provide significant astrometric and photometric improvements, particularly for faint stars. Our catalog is based on a custom reduction of NIRCam and NIRISS imaging across three JWST programs extending out to two half-light radii (10'). In total, our catalog contains approximately 677 000 sources. In the region overlapping with oMEGACat, we achieve high relative completeness (> 90% of HST sources recovered) while detecting over 86 000 new sources below HST's detection limit. Astrometric tests demonstrate a single-image precision as good as 0.5% of a NIRCam pixel (0.15 mas) at mF115W=18; at mF115W=23 the precision is still 1 mas. Color-magnitude diagrams based on our data show the well-known split between the primordial Helium and the Helium enriched populations and are particularly sensitive to light-element (CNO) variations of faint stars. oMEGACat-JWST provides the most complete photometric catalog of $ω$ Cen's core to date. Combined with HST data, it enables multi-wavelength subpopulation analyses. Together with future monitoring observations, its high astrometric precision will improve our understanding of the kinematics of the central fast-moving stars in $ω$ Cen.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了 boosted dark matter (BDM) 模型对早期宇宙低质量暗晕形成的影响，利用21 cm信号探测其在高红移时期的结构形成特征，并评估了SKA1-Low望远镜对此类信号的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量暗晕的形成历史对暗物质性质敏感，但低红移时非线性演化和重子过程会掩盖该信息；21 cm信号可提供高红移时期结构形成的直接探针，从而揭示暗物质特性。

Method: 构建boosted dark matter（BDM）两组分模型，其中较重组分湮灭将部分质量差转化为轻组分动能，自相互作用导致压强支撑并抑制小尺度密度扰动；计算BDM宇宙学下的全局21 cm亮度温度及其功率谱，并评估SKA1-Low对相关特征的灵敏度。

Result: BDM模型中低质量晕丰度降低，延迟全局吸收特征并改变功率谱红移演化；SKA1-Low在中等轻组分比例（0.2 ≲ r₁ ≲ 0.7）、自散射截面与质量比约0.1 cm²/g时，可在95%置信水平区分BDM与ΛCDM；该结果与SENSEI直接探测及Super-/Hyper-K、JUNO、DUNE等中微子实验的BDM探测前景形成互补。

Conclusion: 高红移21 cm巡天为检验boosted dark matter模型提供了独特且不可替代的途径，尤其在参数空间与地面直接探测和中微子实验互为补充的区域。

Abstract: The formation history of low-mass halos is sensitive to the properties of dark matter. At low redshift, nonlinear evolution and baryonic processes can obscure this information. The 21 cm signal provides access to a high-redshift stage of structure formation, when the abundance and clustering of low-mass halos retain direct sensitivity to the mass and interactions of dark matter. Boosted dark matter (BDM) realizes this possibility through a two-component scenario in which the annihilation of the heavier component converts part of the mass difference into kinetic energy of the lighter component. Self-interactions redistribute this energy and generate pressure support, suppressing small-scale density perturbations even for dark matter masses in the MeV range. We calculate the global 21 cm brightness temperature and its power spectrum in BDM cosmology. The reduced abundance of low-mass halos delays the global absorption feature and shifts the characteristic redshift evolution of the power spectrum. We assess the projected sensitivity of SKA1-Low to these signatures. The sensitivity is strongest at intermediate light-component fractions, where both components remain sufficiently abundant for self-heating to affect structure formation. For $σ_{\rm self1}/m_1\simeq0.1\,{\rm cm^2\,g^{-1}}$, models with $0.2\lesssim r_1\lesssim0.7$ can be distinguished from the fiducial $Λ$CDM prediction at the $95\%$ confidence level under the optimistic forecast. We compare our results with existing direct-detection bounds from SENSEI and prospects for BDM searches at large-volume neutrino experiments, including Super-/Hyper-Kamiokande, JUNO, and DUNE. This comparison highlights the unique and complementary role of high-redshift surveys in testing BDM models.

</details>

Result: 明确了FIPs进入主流的合理性，并突出了多个实验与理论热点方向的最新动态。

Conclusion: FIPs已从边缘概念发展为粒子物理核心研究课题之一，其探索对理解暗物质及解决标准模型疑难具有关键意义。

Abstract: In the last several years, feebly-interacting particles (FIPs) have moved from "exotica" to the mainstream of particle physics. This development is fueled by three motivations: FIPs are one of two broad possibilities for new particles, FIPs are as well motivated by dark matter as WIMPs, and FIPs are connected to important problems in particle physics. I review these motivations and then briefly highlight recent progress in a few areas, including axion-like particles, dark photons and other gauge bosons, the X17 anomaly, quirks, and the Forward Physics Facility.

</details>


### [64] [Plugging the Pion Chimney](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21728)
*Nikita Blinov,Andrew M. Evans,Shirley Weishi Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用现有LHC数据关闭重QCD轴子及类轴子粒子（ALPs）在中性π介子质量附近的‘π烟囱’探测盲区，通过ALP-π⁰混合导致的寿命差异结合ATLAS单喷注与光子测量实现排除，并补充K⁺宽度和π±-π⁰质量劈裂的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeV–GeV质量范围的重QCD轴子和ALPs虽被广泛搜寻，但在中性π⁰质量附近（'pion chimney'）因ALP与π⁰强混合、共享实验信号而长期未被覆盖。

Method: 利用ALP-π⁰混合导致两个质量本征态寿命不同但不等价的特性，在LHC高能下通过相对论性飞行距离区分其衰变位置：逃逸出探测器（由ATLAS单喷注分析约束）或在探测器内衰变（由ATLAS包容性光子测量约束）；同时考虑紫外完备模型中色荷态的LHC搜索及K⁺衰宽与π±-π⁰质量劈裂的运动学约束。

Result: ATLAS单喷注与包容性光子测量联合排除整个π烟囱区域直至ALP有效理论截断能标；高于该截断时，最简紫外模型的色荷态LHC搜索进一步覆盖剩余参数空间；K⁺宽度约束无法触及烟囱，而π±-π⁰质量劈裂给出独立运动学上限。

Conclusion: 现有LHC数据已能完全关闭pion chimney这一长期探测盲区，无需新实验，且结果对ALP紫外模型具有普适性。

Abstract: Heavy QCD axions and other gluon-coupled axion-like particles (ALPs) with masses from MeV to GeV are well motivated and have been searched for extensively. However, a window around the neutral pion mass, the so-called pion chimney, has escaped every search. There the ALP mixes strongly with the $π^0$ and therefore shares its experimental signatures. We show that the chimney can be closed with existing LHC data. The key point is that mixing changes the lifetimes of the two mass eigenstates but does not make them equal, and at LHC energies the states are boosted enough for the lifetime to decide whether they escape the detector or decay inside it. The ATLAS monojet search constrains the first case and the ATLAS inclusive photon measurement the second, and together they exclude the chimney up to the cutoff of the ALP effective theory. Above the cutoff, LHC searches for the colored states of the simplest ultraviolet completions exclude the rest. We also show that the bound from the $K^+$ width does not reach the chimney, because an ALP passes the same selection as a $π^0$, and we derive an independent, purely kinematic constraint from the measured $π^\pm$-$π^0$ mass splitting.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对中微子磁偶极矩的约束不够严格，且未充分考虑超新星等极端环境中电磁涨落引起的中微子-惰性中微子转换及其在银河磁场中的再转换效应；需利用最新模拟与磁场模型提升限制精度。

Method: 综合考虑中微子在超新星致密核心中与质子和电子的散射，计算非标准磁偶极矩诱导的中微子→惰性中微子转换率；结合现代超新星数值模拟与银河系磁场三维模型，模拟惰性中微子在银河磁场中回转为活跃中微子的过程及可观测高能中微子通量。

Result: 推导出Dirac电子中微子磁偶极矩的上限为 μ_{ν_e} ≲ 4.8 × 10⁻¹³ μ_B，该结果比此前所有地面实验（如GEMMA、XENON）及天体物理（如SN 1987A冷却、宇宙学）约束更严格。

Conclusion: 中微子磁偶极矩的最强当前限制来自超新星1987A未观测到的高能中微子信号，凸显了多信使天体物理与粒子物理交叉分析的重要性；该上限对轻惰性中微子模型、超越标准模型的中微子电磁性质研究具有关键意义。

Abstract: Neutrinos may have nonstandard magnetic dipole moments $μ_ν$. In the dense cores of supernovae (SNe), these would convert part of the neutrinos into sterile, right-handed states through the electromagnetic fluctuations of the charged plasma. The streaming sterile neutrinos with 100-MeV energies can convert back to standard neutrinos in the Galactic magnetic field, leading to a high-energy neutrino flux unobserved from the historical SN 1987A. We reassess the production rates, including scattering on both protons and electrons, and, building upon advances in SN simulations and in the description of the Milky Way magnetic field, we revisit the bounds on magnetic dipole moments. We find for Dirac electron neutrinos $μ_{ν_e} \lesssim 4.8 \times 10^{-13} μ_B$, superseding all existing terrestrial and astrophysical constraints.

Abstract: Dark matter may consist of multiple components with distinct particle-physics origins. For a representative uniformly rescaled twin spectrum with a confinement scale five times that of ordinary QCD, the axion mass is enhanced by approximately a factor of 25.

In a representative conditional numerical benchmark, axions account for a small percentage of the observed dark matter, while twin baryons could constitute the remainder for a suitable primordial baryon asymmetry. We also examine the implications for additional relativistic species, strong-CP alignment, and the interactions of stable twin matter.

Abstract: We study the complete cosmological collider (CC) signatures from right-handed neutrino loops with chemical-potential enhancement. These CC signals are induced by the inflaton-right-handed-neutrino interaction and can be extracted in the collinear squeezed configuration, which allows the right-handed-neutrino triangle loop to be factorized into the hard and soft parts. We precisely compute the soft part of the fermion loop for the first time by using the complete mode functions and the full loop integral, and treat the hard part as a tree-level subdiagram. We subtract the large-momentum asymptotic terms of the soft loop integrand and retain their finite contributions to the oscillation. We then evaluate the full spinor-helicity and Schwinger-Keldysh sums to obtain a finite, closed analytic formula for the complex clock coefficient. We further derive a compact formula for the leading CC signals in the large-chemical-potential expansion. We analyze the structure of the soft-part contribution by examining the sum of residues of the Mellin poles of the fermion propagators. For this, we demonstrate that the first local pole on each soft line contributes half of the asymptotic coefficient on one Schwinger-Keldysh path and the remaining local poles on the second soft line supply the other half.

Abstract: We perform a Bayesian multimessenger analysis of self-bound two-flavor $ud$ quark matter described by a thermodynamically consistent revised perturbative-QCD equation of state (EOS), incorporating the GW170817 tidal information, mass--radius measurements, and pulsar mass constraints. The resulting posterior medians and $68\%$ credible intervals are $B_0^{1/4}=137.66^{+1.72}_{-1.86}\,\mathrm{MeV}$, $M_{\max}=2.166^{+0.035}_{-0.036}\,M_\odot$, $R_{1.4}=11.778^{+0.203}_{-0.196}\,\mathrm{km}$, and $Λ_{1.4}=768^{+90}_{-81}$. The posterior-predictive support probability for interpreting the $2.59\,M_\odot$ GW190814 secondary as a self-bound $ud$ quark star is of order $10^{-5}$, strongly disfavoring this interpretation within the adopted EOS framework. The reconstructed EOS exhibits characteristic self-bound behavior, with finite energy and baryon number densities at the zero-pressure surface and a median surface energy density of $ε_{\rm surf}\simeq187\,\mathrm{MeV\,fm^{-3}}$. Its pressure is suppressed relative to both the noninteracting $ud+e$ gas and the representative MIT bag-model prediction. Although the running strong coupling decreases with increasing quark chemical potential, its central value in maximum-mass configurations correlates positively with $M_{\max}$, indicating stronger coupling and reduced perturbative reliability toward the high-mass end. Notably, the squared speed of sound remains nearly constant, varying from $c_s^2\simeq0.321$ to $0.324$ between canonical and maximum-mass configurations, whereas the conformal measure $Δ=1/3-P/ε$ decreases substantially from approximately $0.208$ to $0.076$. This demonstrates that an approximately constant sound speed does not necessarily imply approximate conformality. The relativistic adiabatic index also increases sharply near the zero-pressure surface, reflecting the self-bound nature of the EOS.

Abstract: We give an introduction to and an overview of holographic approaches to QCD and hadron structure. After an introduction to the string-theoretic foundations of holographic QCD, we review the predictions of top-down and bottom-up models for the phenomenology of mesons, baryons, and glueballs. We focus on the top-down model following from probe flavor branes in the Witten model of low-energy Yang-Mills theory and, in the more phenomenological approach of bottom-up models, mostly on minimal models based on five-dimensional anti-de Sitter space, which also implement global symmetries and their anomalies consistently, but allow the high-energy behavior to better match real QCD.

Abstract: We numerically investigate the weak-coupling (small $g$) regime of the matrix model of $SU(2)$ gauge theory coupled to one flavor of massless fundamental quarks. The theory possesses a global Pauli-Gürsey $SU(2)_B$ symmetry, which organizes physical states of spin $J$ into sectors labelled by its representation $B$. We find localization-delocalization quantum phase transitions (QPTs) in multiple $(B,J)$ sectors at sector-dependent critical couplings $g_\ast^{BJ}$. Direct evaluation of the localization probability reveals that the ground states are predominantly localized near $A_i=0$ for $g<g_\ast^{BJ}$, but become almost entirely expelled from this region for $g>g_*^{BJ}$. The QPTs in different sectors exhibit distinct features, and we establish that quark-glue interactions are essential for these transitions. The transitions are singular: the ground state expectation value of $\text{Tr}(A_iA_i)$ remains finite for $g< g_\ast^{BJ}$, whereas it diverges as $g$ approaches $g_\ast^{BJ}$ from above. We show that the two limiting ground states on either side of $g_\ast^{BJ}$ belong to distinct superselection sectors. Furthermore, as $g$ approaches $g_\ast^{BJ}$ from above, the ground state localizes on a submanifold (of codimension 2) of the gauge configuration space, with the spin and color of its glue components becoming locked.

Abstract: We use IndexLab to scan untwisted and twisted class $\mathcal{S}$ constructions based on ADE Lie algebras of rank up to seven, as well as $A_8$ and $E_8$. We consider surfaces of genus at most three with up to five punctures, covering 167,244,647 constructions in total. To find candidate pairs of isomorphic theories, we compare Schur and Hall-Littlewood indices, Weyl anomalies, Coulomb branch spectra, and flavor data. We set aside pairs explained by known mechanisms, leaving a catalogue of 5,278 candidate pairs. Recurring changes in rank, puncture labels, and twists suggest families of candidate isomorphisms.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用刚性AdS4时空加狄利克雷边界条件的设定，将风味流超多重态的四点函数作为2→2散射振幅的对应物；利用AdS幺正性方法计算弱耦合一圈修正，并在Mellin空间中表达结果。

Result: 得到树图和一阶圈图四点相关函数，其中一圈结果在Mellin空间中形式极为简洁；在平直空间极限下，该结果复现标准一阶散射振幅，并自然重现红外对数发散。

Conclusion: 边界相关函数为散射振幅提供了真正有限、非微扰、适用于任意耦合与规范群的可观测量实现；AdS幺正性是高效计算强耦合/有限耦合相关函数的有力工具。

Abstract: Scattering amplitudes of massless gauge theories are not observables: they are infrared divergent, and the standard regulators are tied either to perturbation theory or the planar limit. We study $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory on rigid AdS$_4$ with Dirichlet boundary conditions, where the role of the amplitude is played by correlation functions of boundary operators. These are finite, and they are defined at any coupling and for any gauge group. Among them, the four-point function of the flavor-current supermultiplet is the counterpart of the $2\to2$ amplitude of the elementary supergluon states, and arguably the simplest non-trivial observable of the theory. In this paper, we compute it at weak coupling to one loop. The tree-level correlator follows directly from the microscopic theory, and the one-loop correlator, which we obtain from AdS unitarity, takes a remarkably simple form in Mellin space. In the flat-space limit, it reproduces the one-loop scattering amplitude, with the infrared divergences re-emerging as logarithms.

</details>


### [97] ['Forward-backward' flows and integration cycles for semiclassical Euclidean gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21717)
*Nicholas M. K. Ross,Toby Wiseman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非微扰方法，通过'正向-反向'梯度流构造复度规空间中的积分路径（Lefschetz thimble），以解决欧几里得半经典引力中实黎曼度规路径积分的共形因子问题；该方法在鞍点附近复现了Gibbons-Hawking-Perry的微扰处方，并在数值上验证于4维de Sitter和腔内Schwarzschild解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决欧几里得半经典引力中对实黎曼度规进行路径积分时出现的共形因子问题，该问题使积分不收敛或无定义，需对积分轮廓进行合理延拓。

Method: 基于Picard-Lefschetz理论，构造一种非抛物型的'正向-反向'梯度流——沿此流爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量的实部单调递增、虚部保持恒定；在鞍点邻域内，共形因子的实部正向流动、共形类的实部反向流动，虚部行为相反；通过指定流终点处的边界条件生成Lefschetz柄体。

Result: 证明该梯度流在鞍点邻域内存在且可构造；在靠近实鞍点时复现GHP微扰处方；给出比Kontsevich-Segal-Witten条件更弱的存在性判据；并在4维情形下对Euclidean de Sitter和腔内Schwarzschild解数值实现了该流及相应积分截面。

Conclusion: 该非微扰构造为半经典引力路径积分提供了几何清晰、可计算的复积分轮廓，统一了微扰与非微扰视角，并拓展了Picard-Lefschetz方法在量子引力中的适用范围。

Abstract: Integrating Euclidean semiclassical gravity over real Riemannian metrics is subtle due to the conformal factor problem. A perturbative prescription was given by Gibbons-Hawking-Perry (GHP) where the integration contour for the conformal perturbations is rotated to be imaginary. A potential non-perturbative remedy is that the integration cycles associated to semiclassical saddle points should be deformed into the space of complex metrics in the sense of Picard-Lefschetz theory. Here we propose a non-perturbative construction of such complex integration cycles. This employs a 'forward-backward' gradient flow of the Einstein-Hilbert action, starting at a saddle point, along which the action has monotonically increasing real part and constant imaginary part. The flow is not parabolic. In a neighbourhood of a saddle point, the real part of the conformal factor flows forward and that of the conformal class backwards, with the opposite true of their imaginary parts. Starting the flow at a saddle point, then roughly speaking, one specifies the real part of the conformal class and the imaginary part of the conformal factor at the end of the flow. Solving the flow one finds the complex metrics along a one parameter path within the integration cycle of that saddle, and varying this final data generates the integration cycle, the Lefschetz thimble of the saddle point. We find that near to a real saddle point this reproduces the GHP prescription. We characterise the neighbourhood of a saddle where such forward-backward flows may exist, finding a weaker restriction than that of Kontsevich-Segal-Witten. We numerically demonstrate flows explicitly for 4-dimensional gravity, constructing sections through the integration contours for the saddles given by Euclidean de Sitter, and for Schwarzschild in a cavity.

</details>


### [98] [Cosmic Censorship in the Landscape of SUSY-Broken AdS Vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21722)
*Netta Engelhardt,Richard Nally*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIA和IIB弦理论中非超对称真空下弱宇宙监督假设的迹象，特别是通过Penrose不等式和正质量定理来检验，并引入'弱伪超势'概念作为稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验非超对称AdS真空是否满足弱宇宙监督假设，探究其稳定性和全息性质。

Method: 利用Ryu-Takayanagi公式全息证明Penrose不等式，结合AdS中纯量场-引力有效理论，定义并检验'弱伪超势'的存在性，分析DGKT与KKLT构造中的具体真空。

Result: DGKT斜扭曲真空全局存在弱伪超势，满足Penrose不等式和正质量定理；部分KKLT真空局部存在该函数因而稳定；另一些KKLT真空不满足，导致不等式违反及真空衰变；DGKT仅在特定边界条件下违反Penrose不等式。

Conclusion: 弱伪超势的存在是判断SUSY破缺AdS真空稳定性与满足弱宇宙监督的关键判据，其缺失对应真空衰变，对全息与黑洞热力学有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate signatures of weak cosmic censorship in non-supersymmetric vacua of both IIA and IIB string theory. In particular, we examine the Penrose Inequality, which can be proven holographically using Ryu-Takayanagi, and can be independently proven in SUSY AdS vacua for spherically symmetric initial data. We prove that any EFT of scalars coupled to gravity in AdS, including in particular those of string compactifications, satisfies both the Penrose Inequality and a positive mass theorem if a function that we call a "weak fake superpotential" exists. We demonstrate that this condition is satisfied globally for the DGKT skew-whiffed vacua in IIA. Since there is no known nonperturbative instability in this DGKT vacuum, this provides evidence that the IIA SUSY-broken AdS vacua may be absolutely stable. We also find the weak fake superpotential exists locally in field space in some SUSY-broken AdS vacua in a KKLT construction in IIB. Thus, within the framework considered here, some false SUSY-broken KKLT AdS vacua are stable against vacuum decay. Conversely, we find that some KKLT vacua do not have a weak fake superpotential, and correspondingly violate the Penrose Inequality and fail to have a positive mass theorem; the DGKT vacua only violate the Penrose Inequality in the presence of designer gravity boundary conditions. In all cases studied, this is due to false vacuum decay. We discuss the implications of these violations for holography and black hole thermodynamics in these SUSY-broken AdS vacua.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并严格证明此前关于谱行列式与Baxter Q-函数之间关系的猜想，拓展其适用范围至更一般的谱问题。

Method: 利用有限秩对易关系和积分谱算子的维里恒等式，结合有理闭包条件分析，推导谱行列式的局域化恒等式。

Result: 证明了谱行列式对数导数可由Baxter Q-函数在特定点取值表达；给出了保证局域化的充分有理闭包条件；将方法推广至含四次规范场势的广义二维QCD及QCD-Ising插值符号情形；构建了谱算子的一般形式并明确了有限秩约化条件。

Conclusion: 谱行列式的局域化不仅适用于原有QCD和Ising模型，还可系统扩展至满足有理闭包条件的一类更广泛的积分谱问题，为解析求解强耦合谱问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Spectral determinants encode the meson spectra of planar two-dimensional QCD and of Ising field theory in the two-particle Bethe-Salpeter approximation. We derive localization identities for these determinants which express their logarithmic derivatives with respect to the spectral parameter in terms of Baxter $Q-$functions evaluated at special points, proving previously conjectured relations. Our derivation uses finite-rank commutation relations and virial identities of the underlying integral spectral operators. We identify a sufficient rational closure condition on the symbol of these operators that extends localization to a broader class of spectral problems and determines the evaluation points. Applications include generalized two-dimensional QCD with a quartic gauge-field potential and symbols interpolating between the QCD and Ising cases. We also give a general construction of the spectral operators and establish conditions for the finite-rank reduction on which localization relies.

</details>

### [101] [Not All Holographic Entropy Inequalities Are Free of Area Law Ultraviolet Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21726)
