<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并检验关于全息熵锥中线性约束是否全部为超平衡（即无面积律发散）的先前主张。

Method: 分析一个显式的反例，并提出一种简单方法来寻找更多反例；报告了约10^5个反例的特征。

Result: 证实存在非超平衡的全息熵约束，推翻了此前认为所有此类约束都无面积律发散的观点。

Conclusion: 先前关于全息熵约束均为超平衡的主张是错误的，相关多个结论与猜想因此被证伪。

Abstract: In the AdS/CFT correspondence, linear constraints on subsystem entropies, which are necessary conditions for the validity of the Ryu-Takayanagi proposal, define the holographic entropy cone. We disprove a prior claim that all holographic constraints of this type are free of area law divergences (superbalanced). We analyze one explicit counterexample and explain how to easily find more; we report signatures of $\mathcal{O}(10^5)$ counterexamples. Our findings invalidate multiple prior claims and conjectures.

</details>


### [102] [Supersymmetric warped AdS$_3$ solutions in eleven-dimensional supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21727)
*Ruggero Noris*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过变形半BPS的AdS3×S3+×S3−×R2背景，构造了十一维超引力的新超对称解族，分析了不同类型的翘曲及其对应的四形式通量和运动方程约束，并计算了守恒荷、保留的超对称性及Killing超代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建十一维超引力中新的超对称解，拓展对AdS/CFT对应及相关超对称结构的理解。

Method: 对半BPS背景AdS3×S3+×S3−×R2进行类光、类空和类时翘曲变形，同时调整两个三维球面，并求解支持的四形式通量及运动方程约束；进一步计算守恒荷与Killing超代数。

Result: 发现两个球面的变形参数必须相等（但半径可不同）；类光解保留4个实超荷，类空/类时解保留8个，并具有含单个例外因子d(2,1;α)的Killing超代数。

Conclusion: 成功构造出三类新超对称解，揭示了翘曲类型与超对称保留数及超代数结构之间的系统关联，为AdS3背景下超对称分类提供了新例证。

Abstract: We construct new families of supersymmetric solutions of eleven-dimensional supergravity by deforming the half-BPS $\mathrm{AdS}_3\times \mathrm{S}^3_+\times \mathrm{S}^3_-\times\mathbb R^2$ background. We consider lightlike, spacelike and timelike warpings of the anti-de Sitter factor, accompanied by deformations of the two three-spheres, and determine the supporting four-form fluxes and the constraints imposed by the equations of motion. In particular, the deformation parameters of the two spheres are necessarily equal, although their radii need not coincide. We compute the conserved charges and determine the preserved supersymmetries and Killing superalgebras. Generic lightlike solutions preserve four real supercharges, whereas spacelike and timelike solutions preserve eight, with a Killing superalgebra containing a single exceptional $\mathfrak d(2,1;α)$ factor.

</details>


### [103] [Gravity/Thermodynamics Correspondence for Generic Dynamical Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21730)
*Vahid Reza Shajiee,Mohammad Mehdi Sheikh-Jabbari,Vahid Taghiloo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在协变相空间框架下建立了广义动力学时空中的引力-热力学对应关系，推导出适用于任意非稳态爱因斯坦引力解的一般零边界上的局域热力学第一和第二定律。熵变仅来自引力部分，定义为与零边界生成矢量相关的位形荷变，温度为其非仿射参数除以2π；第二定律指出，在满足零能量条件的物质和正非仿射时钟下，沿零矢量方向的熵变严格非负——该结论即使在边界面积减小时仍成立，从而将热力学不可逆性与标准面积定理脱钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统引力热力学对稳态背景、事件视界或精确熵态函数的依赖，建立适用于一般动力学时空的普适热力学定律。

Method: 基于协变相空间形式主义，在一般零边界上针对爱因斯坦引力（含极小耦合物质）的非稳态解，定义物理观测者，并将熵变构造为零边界生成矢量对应的表面荷变，温度取为其非仿射参数（除以2π）。

Result: 确立了适用于任意非稳态解的局域第一、第二热力学定律；熵变为非可积量，但在近稳态极限下退化为Hollands–Wald–Zhang动力熵公式；第二定律不依赖面积增加，即使边界面积减小仍成立。

Conclusion: 引力热力学本质上是局域、动力学且不依赖于稳态假设、事件视界或全局熵函数的存在；其基础可完全由第一性原理（协变相空间与几何结构）导出。

Abstract: Working within covariant phase space formalism, we establish \textit{gravity-thermodynamics correspondence} for generic dynamical spacetimes. We derive local first and second laws of thermodynamics on general null boundaries, for generic non-stationary solutions to Einstein gravity {with minimally coupled matter} for physical observers that we carefully specify. Entropy variation, which receives contribution only from the gravitational sector, is the surface charge variation associated with a vector field generating the null boundary, and temperature is its non-affinity parameter (divided by $2π$). The statement of the second law in our setting is that the entropy variation evaluated along the null vector field is strictly non-negative for the admissible positive-non-affinity clock and matter satisfying the null energy condition. Our dynamical entropy variation, which is non-integrable for generic non-stationary solutions, becomes integrable in near-stationary configurations, reproducing the Hollands--Wald--Zhang dynamical entropy formula. Strikingly, this local second law holds even when the boundary area is decreasing, disentangling thermodynamic irreversibility and the second law from standard area theorems. Our first-principles construction demonstrates that gravitational thermodynamics requires neither a stationary background, teleological event horizons, nor an exact entropy state function on individual codimension-1 spacelike slices of the null boundary (codimension two in spacetime).

</details>


### [104] [Modular Hamiltonian and modular flow of a massive scalar in an interval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21739)
*Raúl E. Arias,Daniel Fondevila*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了自由大质量标量场在任意质量-长度乘积 mL 下区间真空模哈密顿量，并研究其本征态与模流行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场论中真空态的模结构，特别是非共形情形下（如存在质量项）模哈密顿量的精确形式及其物理含义。

Method: 通过求解线性积分方程获得模哈密顿量本征态，利用Neumann级数展开解释反射机制，并借助Painlevé V超越函数确定归一化；进而追踪因果钻石内局域波包的模流演化。

Result: 得到任意mL下区间真空模哈密顿量的精确表达式；本征态由从区间端点发出的质量平面波叠加构成；模流非几何——对易子分布会扩散并在共形轨迹前方出现峰值。

Conclusion: 即使在有质量的自由标量场中，真空模结构仍具有丰富非局域特征，模流显著偏离几何流，揭示了真空纠缠结构的深层动力学。

Abstract: We obtain the vacuum modular Hamiltonian of a free massive scalar in an interval, for any $mL$. Its eigenvectors are superpositions of massive plane waves emitted from the two end points, with amplitudes given by a linear integral equation whose Neumann series is an expansion in reflections, and a normalization fixed by a Painlevé V transcendent. We follow the modular flow of initially localized wave packets inside the causal diamond. The flow is not geometric: the commutator profiles spread and can develop peaks ahead of the conformal trajectory.

</details>


### [105] [Modular conjugations for thermal states and second worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21750)
*Alan Garbarz,Gabriel Palau,Guillem Pérez-Nadal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维共形场论中热态的模共轭算子J，通过显式循环纯化方法计算其作用，发现模像对应实时空点上的场，复坐标仅在将交换子代数外的算符表示为原时空场时出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维CFT中热态的模共轭结构，特别是已知表达式中出现的复坐标x±iβ/2及其物理解释（所谓'第二世界'）的起源与本质。

Method: 采用显式的循环纯化方法：一是通过共形映射到Rindler楔形并利用真空矢量；二是利用局部代数自然锥中的矢量；并对U(1)手性流模型采用福克空间加倍法进行纯化，与Sorce的标准纯化进行比对。

Result: 得到热态下模共轭J的显式公式；证明模像对应实时空点上的场；阐明复坐标的出现是因将交换子代数外的算符强行表示在原时空上所致；确认Fock空间加倍纯化等价于Sorce的标准纯化，并推广至所有初级热态。

Conclusion: 模共轭J的本质作用域是实时空，复坐标并非基本物理输入，而是特定表示选择下的数学副产品；该结果澄清了‘第二世界’的几何含义，统一了不同纯化方案。

Abstract: The modular conjugation $J$ maps an algebra of observables onto its commutant. For the vacuum on a Rindler wedge it is a spacetime reflection, but for thermal states in two-dimensional CFTs the known expressions involve chiral fields at the complex points $x\pm iβ/2$, interpreted as belonging to a ``second world''. Here we compute $J$ for the geometric thermal state in explicit cyclic purifications, using either the vacuum vector, through the conformal map to a Rindler wedge, or a vector in the natural cone of a local algebra. The modular images are then fields at real points, and complex coordinates arise only when operators of the commutant outside the image of the thermal representation are written as fields on the original spacetime. For the chiral $U(1)$ current we also purify by doubling the Fock space, obtain explicit formulas for $J$, show that this purification coincides with Sorce's canonical purification, and treat all primary thermal states.

</details>


### [106] [From Top to Bottom: Quark Mass Hierarchies in Heterotic String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21751)
*Yuri Chervonyi,Fabian Ruehle,Nenad Tomasev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平滑杂化Calabi-Yau紧致化中，标准模型费米子质量层级是否能在树图水平上通过微扰方式生成，并识别出两种新的由复结构模场引发质量层级的机制；发现此前研究的四重二次型模型受阻，最终在大量Calabi-Yau线丛模型数据库中找到唯一满足条件的光滑商空间，并由此得到符合现实的夸克质量层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型费米子质量存在跨越五个数量级的巨大层级，本文旨在探究该层级能否在计算可控的平滑杂化Calabi-Yau紧致化（超引力近似有效）中，于树图水平通过微扰机制自然产生。

Method: 结合全纯超势积分与物质场Kähler度规计算物理Yukawa耦合；前者代数可解，后者需数值求解Ricci平坦度规、Hermitian Yang-Mills联络及调和丛值形式所满足的偏微分方程组；在此框架下解析与数值分析复结构模场驱动质量层级的新机制，并系统扫描数万个完全交截Calabi-Yau线丛模型数据库。

Result: 发现两种新树图机制在已知四重二次型模型中均受阻；在数据库中找到唯一光滑商空间，其在树图水平支持三阶上型夸克Yukawa矩阵；在此流形上计算物理耦合并做重整化群演化，成功再现顶、粲、上、底夸克的现实质量层级。

Conclusion: 费米子质量层级可在平滑杂化Calabi-Yau紧致化的树图水平实现，且依赖复结构模场调控；该结果为弦论唯象学提供了首个在超引力可控区域内实现完整夸克质量谱的明确构造。

Abstract: Standard Model fermion masses exhibit large hierarchies, spanning more than five orders of magnitude in the quark sector alone. In this paper, we study whether these hierarchies can be generated perturbatively at tree level in smooth heterotic Calabi-Yau compactifications, where the supergravity approximation remains under computational control. In such backgrounds, physical Yukawa couplings combine holomorphic superpotential integrals with matter field Kahler metrics. The former can be computed algebraically, while the latter require solving a coupled system of partial differential equations for the Ricci-flat metric, Hermitian Yang-Mills connections, and harmonic bundle-valued forms. Within this framework, we identify two new tree-level mechanisms by which complex structure moduli can generate fermion mass hierarchies and show analytically and numerically that both mechanisms are obstructed for previously studied tetra-quadric models. Motivated by these obstructions, we scan the existing databases of tens of thousands of complete intersection Calabi-Yau line bundle models and find a unique smooth quotient admitting a rank-three up-quark Yukawa matrix at tree level. Evaluating the physical couplings on this manifold and evolving them under the renormalization group, we obtain realistic mass hierarchies for the top, charm, up, and bottom quarks in the smooth supergravity regime.

</details>


### [107] [Giant gravitons without giant gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21754)
*Blake J. Bottesi,James T. Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自由玻色子U(N)多矩阵模型的巨引力子展开，揭示了其与d节点quiver图的对应关系，并暗示了一种广义全息原理的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索没有明显引力对偶的模型是否仍可赋予膜（brane）对偶解释，拓展全息原理的适用范围。

Method: 通过分析自由玻色子U(N)多矩阵模型的配分函数，建立其与d个巨引力子及d节点带双基本场quiver图之间的对应关系。

Result: 发现该模型配分函数可展开为巨引力子形式，每个巨引力子对应一个矩阵，整体结构由d节点quiver刻画；但该展开具有非传统特征——巨引力子配分函数本身具有内禀的N依赖性。

Conclusion: 多矩阵模型虽无明显引力对偶，却可赋予巨引力子（即D-膜）图像，支持更广义的全息思想；但其巨引力子展开因N依赖性而区别于传统AdS/CFT中的情形。

Abstract: We demonstrate that the partition function of a free bosonic $U(N)$ multi-matrix model admits a giant graviton expansion. For $d$ matrices, there are $d$ distinct giant gravitons, and the expansion can be characterized by a $d$-node quiver with bifundamentals connecting the nodes. This suggests the intriguing possibility of a more generalized holography where models without any obvious gravitational dual can nevertheless be given a dual brane interpretation. The giant graviton expansion of this multi-matrix model is somewhat unconventional though, as the giant graviton partition functions will necessarily have intrinsic $N$-dependence.

</details>


### [108] [FLZ integrability in the 't Hooft model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21787)
*Alexey Litvinov,Egor Shestopalov,Nikita Slavnov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了大N_c二维QCD中具有任意不等夸克质量的介子的't Hooft方程，通过Fredholm形式和Baxter差分方程，建立了谱行列式与Baxter Q函数之间的关系，并证明了介子谱的非简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大N_c极限下二维QCD中介子谱的数学结构及其与可积系统的关系。

Method: 利用't Hooft方程的Fredholm形式及关联的Baxter有限差分方程，结合有限秩对易子关系分析Baxter解的特殊点恒等式与谱留数，并推导本征函数归一化因子的精确表达式。

Result: 确立了Fateev-Lukyanov-Zamolodchikov（FLZ）谱行列式恒等式中Baxter表达式的单位留数性质；证明了任意不等夸克质量下介子谱的非简并性；识别出一类具有'FLZ可积性'的更广义积分算子，并将其应用于Ising场论中的介子谱问题。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了Baxter方程解析结构与完全可积Fredholm算子理论之间的深刻联系，为二维强耦合规范理论提供了新的可积性视角。

Abstract: We study the 't Hooft equation describing mesons in large-$N_c$ two-dimensional QCD with arbitrary unequal quark masses. Using its Fredholm formulation and the associated Baxter finite-difference equation, we investigate the relations between spectral determinants and canonical Baxter $Q$-functions. We derive special-point identities for the Baxter solutions by exploiting finite-rank commutator relations of the underlying integral operators. We also determine the spectral residues of the inhomogeneous solutions and obtain exact expressions for eigenfunction normalization factors in terms of values at distinguished complex points. These results establish the unit-residue property of the Baxter expressions entering the Fateev-Lukyanov-Zamolodchikov (FLZ) spectral-determinant identities. As a further application, we prove the nondegeneracy of the meson spectrum for arbitrary unequal quark masses. We further identify a broader class of integral operators exhibiting the same mechanism, which we refer to as FLZ integrability, and illustrate its applicability to the meson spectral problem in Ising field theory. Our approach reveals a connection between the analytic structure of Baxter equations and the theory of completely integrable Fredholm operators.

</details>


### [109] [Simple Models of Thermal Order in $2+1$ Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21842)
*Michael Smolkin,Lev Yung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高温下仍能保持有序的量子场论模型，通过Z2×Z2和Z2×O(n)标量模型结合费米子，展示了即使在任意高温下仍存在自发对称性破缺与长程关联现象，尤其在n≥3时发现加热反而增强空间关联的新奇行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在高温下仍能维持有序态的量子系统，挑战传统认为热涨落会破坏长程有序的认知，并为实验实现提供理论基础。

Method: 采用受控的高温展开方法，并辅以数值泛函重整化群分析，研究Z2×Z2及Z2×O(n)标量场模型与狄拉克费米子的耦合系统。

Result: 在Z2×Z2模型中实现高温下的手征对称性破缺；在Z2×O(2)模型中发现高温BKT超流相；在Z2×O(n≥3)模型中发现有序畴尺寸随温度升高而增大，即加热增强空间关联。

Conclusion: 简单量子场论模型中可存在高温持久序，打破了Mermin-Wagner定理对连续对称性的限制（在特定耦合机制下），为薄层材料中实现热稳定有序态提供了新路径。

Abstract: Heating need not destroy order. We show that it persists to arbitrarily high temperatures in simple quantum field theories in $2+1$ spacetime dimensions. In a $\mathbb Z_2\times\mathbb Z_2$ model of two real scalar fields, a sufficiently negative mixed quartic coupling drives one field to condense, spontaneously breaking its $\mathbb Z_2$ symmetry. When coupled to massless Dirac fermions through a Yukawa interaction, this condensate generates a fermion mass, thereby spontaneously breaking parity and time-reversal symmetries. We extend this construction to a $\mathbb Z_2\times O(n)$ scalar model, in which the Mermin-Wagner theorem precludes spontaneous breaking of the continuous $O(n)$ symmetry at finite temperatures. Nevertheless, for $n=2$, a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless superfluid phase persists to arbitrarily high temperatures. In this phase, spatial correlations decay algebraically, with an exponent that approaches zero as the temperature increases. For $n\geq 3$, we uncover a further unexpected feature of these models: the characteristic size of ordered domains grows asymptotically with temperature, revealing an unusual enhancement of spatial correlations upon heating. We establish our results using a controlled high-temperature expansion, with selected findings corroborated by numerical functional renormalization group analysis. The simplicity of the field content in these models provides a promising starting point for exploring experimental realizations of persistent thermal order in thin-film materials.

</details>


### [110] [Structure Constants of Planar N=4 SYM at Any Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21843)
*Simon Ekhammar,Alessandro Georgoudis,Nikolay Gromov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新表述了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中结构常数的最新猜想，采用分离变量形式便于非微扰分析，并通过量子谱曲线引入状态依赖信息；以两个半BPS算符与Konishi多重态自旋-2成员的三点函数为例，在有限耦合下完成计算，结果覆盖弱到强耦合区域，复现已知微扰与弦论极限，并符合共形引导约束，同时获得新的六阶、七阶微扰系数及高精度强耦合数值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在非微扰框架下分析N=4 SYM中结构常数，需将现有猜想转化为更适于解析处理的形式，尤其是兼容量子谱曲线描述。

Method: 将结构常数猜想重写为分离变量形式，所有状态依赖信息由量子谱曲线编码；结合解析与数值方法，在有限耦合下计算特定三点函数，并进行高精度拟合以提取微扰和强耦合数据。

Result: 成功计算了两个半BPS算符与Konishi自旋-2算符的三点函数，覆盖全耦合范围；复现弱耦合微扰与强耦合弦论极限；满足共形bootstrap边界；获得新六阶、七阶微扰系数及高精度强耦合数据。

Conclusion: 该分离变量形式为N=4 SYM中非微扰三点函数研究提供了有力框架，验证了量子谱曲线在关联函数中的核心作用，并拓展了微扰与强耦合下的精确结果。

Abstract: We recast a recent conjecture for structure constants in planar N=4 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory in a separation-of-variables-inspired form suitable for non-perturbative analysis. All state-dependent information enters through the quantum spectral curve. As a showcase, we compute at finite coupling the three-point functions of two half-BPS operators and the spin-two member of the Konishi multiplet. Our results span weak to strong coupling, reproduce the known perturbative and string-theory limits, and are consistent with conformal-bootstrap bounds. We also obtain new six- and seven-loop perturbative coefficients and extract new strong-coupling data from high-precision numerical fits.

</details>


### [111] [Regular/Singular Black Hole Thermodynamics from Renormalised Volume/Area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22107)
*Gabriel Arenas-Henriquez,Rodrigo Olea,David Rivera-Betancour*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维AdS C度规中全息与拓扑重整化Euclidean作用量之间的有限失配，通过分离曲率正则部分与锥轴上的奇异贡献，并结合Euler归一化，将剩余差异归因于重整化世界面面积的张力加权Euler项，进而将其识别为极点处黑洞视界上的Gauss-Bonnet熵贡献；同时，该差异在固定总弦张力下提供了热力学长度的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT对应中全息与拓扑重整化方法在非光滑时空（如含锥奇点的C度规）下的不一致性及其物理含义，特别是对熵和热力学长度的几何起源的澄清。

Method: 基于共形引力中重整化体积与爱因斯坦扇区的关系，分离正则曲率贡献与锥轴上的分布型贡献；引入光滑流形的Euler归一化；分析重整化世界面面积的张力加权Euler项；关联至Gauss-Bonnet熵与热力学长度。

Result: 发现全息与拓扑重整化Euclidean作用量的残差由张力加权的Euler项决定，该残差即为极点处的Gauss-Bonnet熵；固定总张力下，两重整化世界面面积之差给出相对张力功，从而定义相对热力学长度。

Conclusion: 作用量失配具有清晰的几何与热力学意义：它不仅编码锥奇点处的Gauss-Bonnet熵，还为热力学长度提供了纯几何诠释，强化了弯曲时空热力学与拓扑、几何不变量之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study the finite mismatch between the holographically and topologically renormalised Euclidean actions of the four-dimensional AdS C-metric. Starting from the relation between Renormalised Volume and the Einstein sector of Conformal Gravity, we separate the regular curvature contribution from the contributions supported on the two conical axes. After introducing the usual Euler normalisation for smooth manifolds, the residual difference between the regular action evaluations is determined by the tension-weighted Euler terms of the renormalised worldsheet area. We identify this difference with the Gauss--Bonnet entropy contribution at the polar conical points of the horizon. The ordinary Euler characteristic remains fixed, while the deficits determine how its curvature representation is divided between regular and singular contributions. At fixed total string tension, the difference of the renormalised worldsheet areas per unit Euclidean time determines the relative tension work, giving a geometric interpretation of the relative thermodynamic length.

</details>


### [112] [Non-integrability of open strings via Pohlmeyer reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22570)
*Divania Carelse,Laila Tribelhorn,Konstantinos Zoubos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Pohlmeyer约化方法研究了经典弦的可积与非可积边界条件，验证了Y=0非极大巨引力子上的弦对应sine-Gordon模型并满足Ghoshal-Zamolodchikov可积性判据；重点分析了AdS3上开弦，利用该判据区分其可积与非可积情形，并指出Pohlmeyer约化有助于理解开弦非可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解经典弦在不同边界条件下的可积性，特别是AdS3中开弦的非可积行为，需借助有效工具如Pohlmeyer约化进行系统判别。

Method: 采用Pohlmeyer约化方法，将弦理论问题映射为低维场论模型（如sine-Gordon），并应用Ghoshal-Zamolodchikov边界可积性判据进行分析。

Result: 确认Y=0非极大巨引力子上弦的Pohlmeyer场满足可积性条件；对AdS3开弦，成功用该判据区分出可积与非可积情形，与近期解析和数值结果一致。

Conclusion: Pohlmeyer约化为判断开弦边界可积性提供了有力且普适的视角，尤其适用于AdS背景下的非可积性分析。

Abstract: We study integrable and non-integrable boundary conditions for classical strings from the point of view of Pohlmeyer reduction. We first consider the integrable case of strings ending on Y=0 non-maximal giant gravitons. Pohlmeyer reduction of such strings leads to a sine-Gordon model, for which a criterion for boundary integrability was provided by Ghoshal and Zamolodchikov. We confirm that the Pohlmeyer field satisfies this condition. We then turn to the main focus of our work, which is open strings on AdS3. By evaluating the Ghoshal-Zamolodchikov criterion for the corresponding Pohlmeyer-reduced models, we are able to distinguish between integrable and non-integrable cases, in line with expectations from recent analytic and numerical studies. We conclude that Pohlmeyer reduction can provide a useful perspective on open-string non-integrability.

</details>


### [113] [Higher Spin Lagrangians for Black Holes in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22594)
*Francesco Campanella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意维度下旋转物体的经典引力多极结构，通过分析高自旋拉格朗日量与引力的耦合，发现高自旋极限下最小多极展开与维度无关，并可复现四维Kerr解；同时找到了一组与维度无关的非最小耦合系数关系，能复现任意维度下的Myers-Perry多极结构，并将分析推广至含不同杨表表示的场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解旋转天体在任意维度下的经典引力多极结构，特别是如何从高自旋场论出发统一描述Kerr和Myers-Perry解的多极特征。

Method: 分析任意维度D=d+1下、由完全对称张量（自旋s）描述的高自旋场与引力的最小及非最小耦合；考察高自旋极限s≫1行为；推广至具有不同杨图表示的高维场。

Result: 发现高自旋极限下最小多极展开维度无关且对应四维Kerr解；找到一组维度无关的非最小耦合系数关系，可精确复现任意维度Myers-Perry黑洞的多极矩；并将结论拓展到更一般的高维场表示。

Conclusion: 高自旋极限提供了一种维度无关的框架，统一刻画旋转黑洞的多极结构；非最小耦合的特定关系是实现Myers-Perry多极结构的关键；结果支持高自旋理论作为探索高维引力几何性质的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the classical gravitational multipolar structure of rotating objects studying higher-spin Lagrangians coupled to gravity in arbitrary dimensions $D=d+1$. We first consider fields described by totally symmetric tensors with $s$ indices. At finite spin, the minimal coupling depends on both the spin and spacetime dimension. We show that, in the high-spin limit $s\gg 1$, the minimal multipolar expansion becomes dimension independent and it corresponds to the one which reproduces the Kerr solution in four dimensions. Furthermore, we identify a dimension independent relation among the non-minimal coupling coefficients that allows us to reproduce the Myers-Perry multipolar structure in arbitrary dimensions. We then extend our analysis to Lagrangians involving fields with different Young representations, which are allowed in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [114] [Lie-Poisson Formulation of Dissipative Spin Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22615)
*Zi-Hao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了流体流动与自旋之间的耦合机制，指出协方差和热力学第二定律无法唯一确定这种耦合，而自旋对易关系与庞加莱代数可固定其形式；可逆情况下流体不施加扭矩，自旋模长守恒、方向按Fermi-Walker方式输运，剪切-自旋耦合源于参考标架形变；不可逆（耗散）效应则在临界剪切率以上使自旋偏离涡度方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差和热力学第二定律不足以唯一确定流体流动与自旋的耦合形式，因为可逆耦合不产生熵；需从更基本的对称性（如自旋代数与庞加莱代数）出发构建理论框架。

Method: 基于自旋算符的对易关系与庞加莱代数的对称性约束，结合相对论连续介质力学与自旋输运几何（如Fermi-Walker输运），区分可逆与不可逆贡献，分析一阶耗散项对自旋取向的影响。

Result: 推导出可逆情形下自旋模长守恒、方向由Fermi-Walker输运决定，剪切-自旋耦合对应于局部标架形变；首次明确一阶耗散导致的剪切-自旋耦合可在临界剪切率以上使自旋偏离局域涡度方向。

Conclusion: 流体-自旋耦合由基本对称性（而非热力学或协方差）决定；可逆与不可逆机制具有本质不同的几何与动力学特征，尤其在强剪切下的自旋重取向现象中体现关键物理意义。

Abstract: Covariance and the second law cannot fix how fluid flow couples to spin, since reversible couplings produce no entropy. We show that the spin commutation relations and the Poincaré algebra fix these couplings. Reversibly, a flow exerts no torque on the spin: its magnitude per particle is conserved, its direction is Fermi--Walker transported, and spin--shear couplings reflect a deforming triad. The magnitude changes only through higher multipoles, as in spinor condensates, or dissipation, whose first-order spin--shear coupling reorients the spin away from the vorticity above a critical shear rate.

</details>


### [115] [Spectral recurrences and maximal analyticity: cosmological correlators at one and two loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22687)
*Bruce A. Bassett,Sergio L. Cacciatori,Ugo Moschella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大解析性和微分-差分方程的de Sitter时空边界关联函数计算方法，通过Mehler-Fock变换将问题转化为谱参数上的代数递推，并给出物理解的解析条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统坐标依赖方法在de Sitter时空边界关联函数计算中的局限性，发展一种完全坐标无关、具严格解析基础的新方法。

Method: 利用两点函数的最大解析性及Schwinger-Keldysh图满足的微分-差分方程；将顶点积分解析延拓至纯虚Lobachevskij圈；对不变气泡核作Mehler-Fock变换，将微分-差分方程转化为谱参数上的代数递推；求解齐次与非齐次递推关系。

Result: 得到边界in-in图的完整解，包括显式的特解（以广义超几何函数表示）和物理解所需的解析选择条件；导出多圈链图的谱乘子由单圈模块与中间分支求和决定；方法在Poincaré坐标下可还原为标准结果。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了de Sitter边界理论中关联函数的一种内在、坐标无关的谱表示框架，统一处理各类Schwinger-Keldysh分支，为宇宙学量子涨落计算奠定严格解析基础。

Abstract: We develop a method for computing de Sitter boundary correlators based on the maximal analyticity of the two-point function and differential--difference equations satisfied by Schwinger--Keldysh diagrams. The vertex integrations are analytically continued through the relevant de Sitter tuboids onto purely imaginary Lobachevskij cycles. Taking the Mehler--Fock transform of the invariant bubble kernels on these Riemannian cycles converts their differential--difference equations into algebraic recurrences in the spectral parameter. The mixed-branch kernel obeys a homogeneous recurrence, whereas the chronological and antichronological kernels satisfy sourced recurrences whose local terms are determined by dimensionally continued tadpoles. We solve these recurrences, constructing explicit particular solutions in terms of generalised hypergeometric functions and identifying the analytic conditions selecting their physical completion. Attachment to the external boundary points then yields an intrinsic spectral representation and a complete solution of the boundary in--in diagrams considered here. Our method is fully coordinate-independent. Specialising to a Poincaré chart readily gives expressions that can be compared directly with those obtained by the standard coordinate-dependent approach. Composing two or more bubbles yields a multi-loop chain whose spectral multiplier is determined by the one-loop building blocks and the sum over intermediate Schwinger--Keldysh branches.

</details>


### [116] [Fluid/Gravity Correspondence in 3d Flat Gravity with Non-Compact Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22718)
*H. Adami,R. Espíndola,K. Lara,A. Latifi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非紧致空间边界的三维平坦引力理论，发现其电荷代数分裂为两个对易的手性流代数，并通过流体/引力字典映射到二维共形流体的应力张量；边界条件分析揭示了手性扭结与Pöschl-Teller算子的联系，并建立了第一定律关系；最后讨论了基于Weyl C*代数的量子化方案及非微扰的√(T T̄)型应力张量径向流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维平坦引力在非紧致边界下的对称结构与物理诠释，尤其是如何通过流体/引力对应建立低维共形流体描述，并探索边界条件、可积性与量子化之间的联系。

Method: 分析受限相空间上的3D平坦引力，推导电荷代数结构；建立流体/引力字典映射；研究Heisenberg型和Dirichlet型边界条件下的手性解与散射理论；利用Schrödinger算子与Pöschl-Teller势分析全局数据；构建Weyl C*代数框架进行量子化；推导并积分非微扰的√(T T̄)型应力张量径向流方程。

Result: 电荷代数分裂为两个对易手性流代数；能量-动量有下界约束；在直线上电荷有限性导致电流为平方可积；全局数据由Schrödinger算子散射变量而非单值性决定；Dirichlet边界给出反射无Pöschl-Teller手性扭结；两类边界均满足第一定律；存在Weyl C*代数量子化方案；应力张量满足精确√(T T̄)型径向流，积分后对应有限径向洛伦兹提升。

Conclusion: 三维平坦引力在非紧边界下展现出丰富的手性结构与可积性，其经典与量子描述可通过共形流体、散射理论和C*代数统一刻画，且应力张量径向演化具有精确非微扰解，暗示深层几何与量子引力联系。

Abstract: We study 3d flat gravity on a restricted phase space with a non-compact spatial boundary. The charge algebra splits into two commuting chiral current algebras, which a fluid/gravity dictionary maps to the stress tensor of a two-dimensional conformal fluid, with energy bounded below by the momentum. On the line, finiteness of the charges selects integrable, square-integrable currents, and the global data are carried by the scattering variables of a Schrödinger operator rather than by a monodromy. We analyze Heisenberg-like and Dirichlet-like boundary conditions; the latter give independently propagating chiral kinks associated with the reflectionless Pöschl-Teller operator. Both admit first-law relations. We outline the quantization as a Weyl $C^*$-algebra with quasi-free states. Retaining the radial dependence, the quasi-local stress tensor obeys an exact, non-perturbative flow of square-root $T\bar T$ type, which integrates to a finite boost in the radial position.

</details>


### [117] [Toric Calabi-Yau orientifolds: constructions, fixed loci and O-plane charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22785)
*Xu Cao,Xin Gao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过除子交换和坐标反射系统构建toric orientifold Calabi-Yau三维流形的方法，并结合新计算与显式例子，深化了对固定点集几何、orientifold荷数据及奇异性的理解，为D膜构型、通量紧致化与模稳定化提供几何输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持orientifold星表的持续更新，并为D膜构型、通量紧致化与模稳定化提供可靠的几何基础。

Method: 基于齐次坐标与格点描述的对应关系，开展逐三角剖分分析，识别单个三角剖分上多个相容对合及其在不同三角剖分间的实现，结合新计算与显式例子分析固定点集几何与orientifold荷数据。

Result: 揭示了toric相内及相间orientifold结构；明确了固定点集几何与orientifold荷；给出了具有不可避免奇点的恒等与不变超曲面族的坐标表示。

Conclusion: 该系统性分析与新结果不仅完善了toric orientifold Calabi-Yau三重流形的构造框架，也为相关弦论唯象学应用提供了关键几何输入。

Abstract: We review the systematic construction of toric orientifold Calabi-Yau threefolds through divisor exchanges and coordinate reflections. We relate the homogeneous-coordinate and lattice descriptions and explain the computation of fixed loci and the geometric data entering Hodge splittings and tadpole conditions. Our triangulation-wise analysis identifies multiple compatible involutions on a single triangulation and tracks their realisations across different triangulations, revealing the orientifold structure within and across toric phases. We complement this review with new calculations and explicit examples that refine the fixed-locus geometry and the associated orientifold charge data. The examples also identify coordinate representatives of the identity and invariant hypersurface families with unavoidable singularities. These results support an ongoing update of the orientifold catalogue and provide geometric input for D-brane configurations, flux compactifications and moduli stabilisation.

</details>


### [118] [5d $\mathbb{Z}_{n}$ S-folds, quotient curves, and isogenies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22811)
*Sung-Soo Kim,Song Liu,Mudassar Sabir,Futoshi Yagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了Z3、Z4和Z6 S-fold下5维秩1超共形理论的Seiberg-Witten曲线，通过镜像曲线实现S-fold，并与E6、E7、E8理论的质量参数特化结果一致；曲线族按su(2)和g2李代数特征组织，并提出利用循环n-同源性统一描述秩1 S-fold曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Z2 S-fold情形至更高阶Z3、Z4、Z6 S-fold，探究其对质量参数的更强约束及几何实现机制。

Method: 在镜像曲线层面系统实现S-fold操作；将秩1 E6、E7、E8理论的质量参数复化并依7-膜插入动机设定全纯性条件；将所得曲线化为Weierstrass形式；分析椭圆曲线族的李代数特征结构；引入循环n-同源性构造统一商曲线描述。

Result: 成功构造Z3、Z4、Z6 S-fold的Seiberg-Witten曲线；两套构造（镜像S-fold与E6/E7/E8参数特化）在Weierstrass形式下完全一致；含连续质量变形的曲线族对应su(2)和g2的特征标；提出n-同源性可统一描述所有Zn S-fold曲线。

Conclusion: Z3/Z4/Z6 S-fold可通过镜像曲线S-fold或高秩E-型理论质量参数特化等价实现；其几何结构由李代数特征标刻画；循环n-同源性为秩1 S-fold提供了普适的商曲线框架。

Abstract: We construct Seiberg--Witten curves for the $\mathbb{Z}_3$, $\mathbb{Z}_4$, and $\mathbb{Z}_6$ S-folds of 5d rank-1 superconformal theories. Compared with the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ case arXiv:2611.09804, $\mathbb{Z}_{3,4,6}$ S-folds require stronger constraints on the mass parameters. We provide a systematic procedure of realizing the S-foldings at the mirror curve level. We obtain the same elliptic curve families by specializing the complexified mass parameters of the rank-1 $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$ theories, with holonomies motivated by the 7-brane insertions required by the S-fold prescription. The two constructions agree in Weierstrass form. The families with continuous mass deformations organize into characters of $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{g}_2$, consistently with their singular fibers. Finally, we discuss that the Seiberg--Witten curves for $\mathbb{Z}_n$ S-folds can be obtained through cyclic $n$-isogenies, which may provide a uniform quotient curve description of rank-1 S-folds.

</details>


### [119] [Strange-Metal Magnetotransport without a Strange-Metal Ground State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22985)
*Yongjun Ahn,Matteo Baggioli,Hyun-Sik Jeong,Keun-Young Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出中间临界性（intermediate criticality）作为奇异金属输运行为的新机制，通过全自洽的全息模型实现了电阻率ρxx∼T和霍尔角余切cotΘH∼T²两种标度律，并指出这些标度仅在由动量弛豫与磁场共同决定的有限温区中出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属中线性温度依赖的电阻率和二次方温度依赖的霍尔角余切虽被广泛归因于量子临界性，但既无严格可控理论支持其在零温不动点共存，实验上也仅在有限温区内观测到；因此需寻找替代机制。

Method: 采用一类极小全息模型，进行完全背反应（fully backreacted）计算，构建具有中间标度几何的临界区域，并分析动量弛豫和外加磁场B对温度窗口上下界的调控作用。

Result: 成功实现ρxx∼T与cotΘH∼T²在同一有限温区共存；该温区上界由动量弛豫设定，下界为Tmin∼B^{2/3}（含对数修正）；在Tmin处，输运标度、几何标度与临界热力学行为同步破缺。

Conclusion: 中间临界性是一种区别于零温量子临界的新奇机制，可解释奇异金属输运；其关键判据是在磁场下输运与热力学标度行为同步消失。

Abstract: Strange metals display a linear-in-temperature resistivity, $ρ_{xx}\sim T$, and a quadratic inverse Hall angle, $\cotΘ_H\sim T^2$. These seemingly universal laws are often attributed to quantum criticality, yet no controlled theory realizes both at an asymptotic zero-temperature fixed point, and experimentally they persist only over finite temperature windows. Here, we show that these properties can arise from an intermediate critical regime that does not govern the ground state. Using a minimal class of holographic models, we construct a fully backreacted realization of both scalings, which appear within a finite temperature window controlled by an emergent intermediate scaling geometry. The window is bounded by relevant deformations of the corresponding fixed point: momentum relaxation sets the upper edge, while the magnetic field $B$ closes it from below at $T_{\min}\sim B^{2/3}$, up to logarithmic corrections. At $T_{\min}$, strange-metal transport, the scaling geometry, and critical thermodynamics break down together. Our results point to intermediate criticality as a distinct route to strange-metal transport, and suggest that it can be identified by the simultaneous breakdown of transport and thermodynamic scaling under a magnetic field.

</details>


### [120] [An Effective Theory for Hot Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23169)
*Julien Barrat,Alessio Miscioscia,Fedor K. Popov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有热屏蔽理论中空间共形线缺陷的高温有效理论，推导出缺陷配分函数的局部几何展开，并计算了多种理论模型中的有效系数，与蒙特卡洛模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高温下空间共形线缺陷的行为，特别是在具有热屏蔽效应的量子场论中，理解其几何响应和统计性质。

Method: 利用重态缺陷矩阵元的收敛体部OPE，推导缺陷一阶系数与体部OPE系数平均乘积的渐近关系；结合ε-展开、大N展开及蒙特卡洛模拟进行计算与验证。

Result: 得到了扭曲自由标量、O(N) Wilson-Fisher临界点、大N Gross-Neveu模型及N=2 Chern-Simons-物质理论中线缺陷的有效张力与弯曲响应系数；三维Ising模型的蒙特卡洛结果与ε-展开Padé插值得到的周长系数高度吻合。

Conclusion: 高温有效理论成功描述了空间共形线缺陷的几何响应，为理解强耦合缺陷系统提供了通用框架，并在多个具体模型中获得定量验证。

Abstract: We develop a high-temperature effective theory for spatial conformal line defects in theories with thermal screening. The defect partition function admits a local geometric expansion, whose leading terms describe the effective tension and bending response of the line. Using a convergent bulk OPE for heavy-state defect matrix elements, we derive asymptotic relations for averaged products of defect one-point coefficients and bulk OPE coefficients in the regime $1 \ll Δ_χ\ll Δ^{1/d}$. We compute the leading EFT coefficients for a twisted free scalar and for the magnetic line at the $\mathrm{O}(N)$ Wilson-Fisher fixed point, large-$N$ Gross--Neveu model and $\mathcal N = 2$ Chern-Simons-matter theory. For the three-dimensional Ising model, Monte Carlo simulations yield a perimeter coefficient in good agreement with a Padé interpolation of our $\varepsilon$-expansion result.

</details>


### [121] [The Pfaffian sector of $\mathrm{SO}(2N)$ BPS cohomology: brane excitations, triality and fortuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23173)
*Zheng Miao,Yuezhang Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中SO(2N)规范群在BMN扇区的1/16-BPS上同调，特别关注含ε张量的算符（Pfaffian扇区），并证明其在所有微扰阶下受保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中非引力类算符（特别是Pfaffian扇区）的BPS结构及其在不同规范群（如SO(8)、SO(7)）下的行为与保护性。

Method: 利用1/16-BPS上同调分析、SU(3)_R表示分解、O(2N)反射对称性、triality对称性及子代数限制方法，结合BMN扇区算符构造与覆盖空间技术。

Result: 发现SO(8)下Pfaffian扇区中存在一个特殊单态（来自ε-张量四元族），及其与triality配对的反射偶类；二者及该四元族在所有N≥4时均全阶受保护；但限制到SO(7)时因量子修正与限制不交换而被提升。

Conclusion: Pfaffian扇区中存在新的非引力BPS类，其保护性依赖于整体群结构，不能简单通过子群限制推断；揭示了对称性、上同调与量子修正之间深刻的非交换关系。

Abstract: We study the classical $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS cohomology of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory with gauge group $\mathrm{SO}(2N)$ in the BMN sector, including the operators built with one $ε$-tensor. These operators are odd under the reflection in $\mathrm{O}(2N)$, carry the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ charge of a D3-brane wrapped on $\mathbb{RP}^3\subset\mathbb{RP}^5$, and form the Pfaffian sector. For $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ we determine this sector through level $j=18$, where the level is twice the total charge of an operator. All but one of its $106$ $\mathrm{SU}(3)_R$ multiplets are products of trace gravitons and excited Pfaffians. The exception is a singlet, represented by the singlet projection of the rank-four member of a four-term family of $ε$-tensor operators. We construct a covering space for the Pfaffian sector in which this family is closed at every rank, and the family defines a non-graviton class for every $N\ge 4$. Triality pairs the singlet with a reflection-even class that is fortuitous with respect to the trace covering. The two classes restrict, along the spinor and the vector embedding of $\mathfrak{so}(7)$ respectively, to the known non-graviton singlet of $\mathrm{SO}(7)$, which is lifted at first order. We show that the two $\mathrm{SO}(8)$ classes, and the family for every $N\ge 4$, are protected to all orders of perturbation theory. Their protection is compatible with the lifting in $\mathrm{SO}(7)$ because restriction to a subalgebra does not commute with the quantum corrections to the supercharge.

</details>


### [122] [StringQFT: A Multiuse Textbook Resource for AI-Assisted Theoretical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23225)
*Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了StringQFT——一个面向弦论构建量子场论的教科书集合及AI辅助理论物理基础设施，包含18卷、190章、3795页内容，配套开放评测基准、检索增强生成支持、训练数据示例和19个可调用计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为推动AI在理论物理（特别是弦论与量子场论交叉领域）中的应用，构建结构化、可检索、可计算、可验证的知识基础设施。

Method: 构建大规模、结构化、带标注的弦论教科书集合（StringQFT），集成开放评测基准、章节级源链接与PDF定位、本地词法检索器、带输入/输出定义的计算工具集，并提供训练数据准备范例和RAG支持。

Result: 发布含18卷、190章、3795页的StringQFT教科书集合；建立含1073项（499项可自动验证）的开放评测基准；提供1371个可检索章节记录及21个源链接训练示例；集成19个有限域计算工具并演示其与教材推导的关联。

Conclusion: StringQFT为AI驱动的理论物理研究提供了首个系统性、可验证、可扩展的知识与工具基础设施，显著提升了大模型在该领域的可解释性、准确性与实用性。

Abstract: We present StringQFT as a textbook collection for string constructions of quantum field theory and as infrastructure for AI-assisted theoretical physics. The collection comprises 18 volumes, 190 chapters and 3,795 pages, covering brane probes, brane webs, geometric engineering, F-theory constructions, wrapped M5-branes and exact worldsheet constructions. Its open-book benchmark inventory contains 1,073 items, including 499 with automatic finite-answer checking. For retrieval-augmented generation, it provides 1,371 section records linked to textbook sources and PDF locations, together with a local lexical retriever. Training-data preparation is illustrated by 21 private, source-linked examples. For agents, it exposes 19 calculation tools with specified inputs and finite domains, connecting tool use to textbook derivations. We illustrate these uses through specific questions, retrieved passages and finite calculations. The textbook collection and accompanying resources are available at https://github.com/xingyang-yu/StringQFT.

</details>


### [123] [CFTs with Large Virasoro Primary Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23231)
*Ziming Ji,Jiahua Tian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个具有唯一真空且所有自旋Virasoro主态间隙大于2的二维紧致幺正玻色型全共形场论（CFT）的显式例子，采用基于高最小范数偶单模格的非对称轨道构造，并通过特定对称性配分实现大间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有大主态间隙的紧致幺正CFT，以探索量子引力边界、模不变性极限及CFT分类问题。

Method: 利用偶单模格构造格CFT，施加结合正交作用与独立符号翻转的非对称轨道操作，消除低维标量主态；严格证明配分无反常，并精确分析未扭曲谱与所有扭曲扇区以确定间隙。

Result: 得到多个显式例子：由三元二次剩余码格导出的无限族，中心荷(c_L,c_R)=(p+1,p+1)（p≥71且p≡23 mod 24）时间隙为3；由Barnes-Wall格导出的无限族，在(c_L,c_R)=(2^m,2^m)（奇数m≥7）时间隙为4；另有两个孤立例子：(48,48)间隙3、(512,512)间隙4。

Conclusion: 首次实现了对任意大的中心荷构造具有固定大间隙（3或4）的紧致幺正玻色CFT，验证了大间隙CFT的存在性，并提供了系统性构造方法。

Abstract: We construct the first explicit examples of compact unitary bosonic full conformal field theories (CFTs) in two dimensions with a unique vacuum and an all-spin Virasoro-primary gap greater than two. Our constructions use asymmetric orbifolds of lattice CFTs built from even unimodular lattices of sufficiently large minimum norm, ensuring that no charged state lies below the desired gap. The gauged symmetries combine a common orthogonal action on the left- and right-moving currents with independent sign reversals; the latter project out the dimension-two scalar primary $\sum_i J_L^iJ_R^i$ that survives when the symmetry acts identically on both chiral halves. We prove that the gauging is anomaly-free and determine the exact gaps by analyzing the invariant untwisted spectrum and all twisted sectors. From lattices constructed using ternary quadratic-residue codes, we obtain an infinite family with gap three at $(c_L,c_R)=(p+1,p+1)$ for primes $p\ge71$ with $p\equiv23\pmod{24}$. From Barnes-Wall lattices, we obtain an infinite family with gap four at $(c_L,c_R)=(2^m,2^m)$ for odd $m\ge7$. We also obtain two additional examples, with gap three at $(c_L,c_R)=(48,48)$ and gap four at $(c_L,c_R)=(512,512)$.

</details>
